📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期麻疹病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 我们发现,大多数病毒传播事件起源于犹他州西南部和亚利桑那州莫哈维县,传播 模式随着时间的推移而变化。0 个 SNP 的阈值可识别具有高灵敏度和特异性的流行病学相关对。家庭、学校和体育运动暴露与 传播 联系独立相关,家庭之间距离的增加降低了联系的可能性。
  • 病毒 基因组 分析显示,显着的 A 至 G 和 U 至 C 取代选择性地集中在基质基因中,尸检分析表明病毒在多个脑区域广泛传播和持续多样化,而外周尿液样本中含有最小突变的假定祖先谱系。
  • 主要结果显示,2026年初麻疹病例和死亡人数显着增加,疑似病例50,500例,确诊或疑似麻疹相关死亡415例。大多数病例发生在五岁以下儿童,其中最集中在达卡区。该研究还报告了常规免疫覆盖率的下降和 SIA 的缺乏,这导致了 麻疹 的死灰复燃。
  • 麻疹 是一种高度传染性的空气传播病毒性疾病,其特征是高基本繁殖数 (R0≈12‐18),可导致严重的肺部和神经系统并发症、长期免疫。 [10.1080/23744235.2026.2762031]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • We identified that most viral dispersal events originated in Southwest Utah and Mohave County, Arizona, with transmission patterns changing over time.
  • Viral genome analysis revealed striking A-to-G and U-to-C substitutions selectively concentrated in the matrix gene, and post-mortem analysis demonstrated widespread dissemination and continued viral diversification in multiple brain regions, whereas a peripheral urine sample contained a minimally mutated putative ancestral lineage.
  • The main results show a significant increase in measles cases and deaths in early 2026, with 50,500 suspected cases and 415 confirmed or suspected measles-related deaths.
  • In the multivariable model, age below 9 months (aOR 0.09, 95% CI 0.03-0.268, p < 0.001) was independently associated with lower odds of vaccination, while household income at or above the median (aOR 3.983, 95% CI 1.8-8.813, p = 0.004) was associated with higher odds.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Acta Neuropathologica  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-11  |  DOI: 10.1007/s00401-026-03116-0  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 152, 1, 68
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

新城疫病毒的 麻疹 样超突变在 BCMA × CD3 T 细胞重定向免疫治疗期间导致致命性脑炎。

Measles-like hypermutation of a Newcastle disease virus drives fatal encephalitis during BCMA × CD3 T-cell-redirecting immunotherapy.
作者： Christina Krueger, Christoph Schaefers, Thomas Guenther, Lisa Leypoldt, Ricardo Ahrens, Winfried Alsdorf, Maximilian Al-Bazaz, Katja Weisel, Sofie-Katrin Kadel, Leo RASCHE
摘要中文翻译摘要 新城疫病毒(NDV 或 AOaV-1)是一种禽副粘病毒,通常仅引起人类轻微的自限性疾病。在这里,我们报告了一名多发性骨髓瘤患者在接受基于 teclistamab 的一线 治疗 和自体干细胞移植后接受 teclistamab、达雷妥尤单抗和来那度胺维持治疗的致命性神经侵袭性 AOaV-1 感染。在快速进展的脑干-小脑脑炎和常规微生物检测阴性后,小脑活检的无偏宏基因组 RNA 测序鉴定出脑实质内高丰度的 AOaV-1。病毒 基因组 分析显示,显着的 A 至 G 和 U 至 C 取代选择性集中在基质基因中,与 ADAR 介导的 RNA 编辑一致。长读长测序解析了两种遗传上不同、独立超突变的 CNS 单倍型。尸检分析表明,病毒在多个大脑区域广泛传播和持续多样化,而外周尿液样本中含有最低限度突变的假定祖先谱系。这些发现支持外周感染,随后是 CNS 播种并在 宿主 进化 内划分。选择性基质基因超突变与 亚急性硬化性全脑炎 中的持续 麻疹病毒 感染密切相关,揭示了副粘病毒适应 CNS 的共同机制。因此,与免疫治疗相关的严重免疫功能障碍可能会创造一个生态位,使通常低致病性的人畜共患病毒能够持续存在、进化并获得严重的神经毒力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查通常低致病性的人畜共患病毒在免疫治疗期间通过免疫功能障碍获得严重神经毒力的可能性,正如新城疫病毒在多发性骨髓瘤患者中引起的致命性脑炎所强调的那样。
设计与对象
该 病例报告 描述了一名多发性骨髓瘤患者在基于 teclistamab 的一线 治疗 和自体干细胞移植后接受 teclistamab、daratumumab 和来那度胺维持治疗的由新城疫病毒 (AOaV-1) 引起的致命性神经侵袭性感染。
核心方法
小脑活检的无偏宏基因组 RNA 测序用于鉴定 AOaV-1,长读长测序用于解析两种遗传上不同、独立超突变的 CNS 单倍型。
主要结果
病毒 基因组 分析显示,显着的 A 至 G 和 U 至 C 取代选择性地集中在基质基因中,尸检分析表明病毒在多个脑区域广泛传播和持续多样化,而外周尿液样本中含有最小突变的假定祖先谱系。
解释与创新
选择性基质基因超突变与 亚急性硬化性全脑炎 中的持续 麻疹病毒 感染密切相关,揭示了副粘病毒适应 CNS 的共同机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,与免疫治疗相关的严重免疫功能障碍可能会产生微环境,使通常低致病性的人畜共患病毒能够持续存在、进化并获得严重的神经毒力。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于单个病例报告,这限制了普遍性,并且证据来自于尸检分析,可能无法完全代表体内动态。

Measles-like hypermutation of a Newcastle disease virus drives fatal encephalitis during BCMA × CD3 T-cell-redirecting immunotherapy.

Authors: Christina Krueger, Christoph Schaefers, Thomas Guenther, Lisa Leypoldt, Ricardo Ahrens, Winfried Alsdorf, Maximilian Al-Bazaz, Katja Weisel, Sofie-Katrin Kadel, Leo RASCHE
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Newcastle disease virus (NDV or AOaV-1) is an avian paramyxovirus that typically causes only mild, self-limiting disease in humans. Here, we report fatal neuroinvasive AOaV-1 infection in a patient with multiple myeloma receiving maintenance therapy with teclistamab, daratumumab, and lenalidomide following frontline teclistamab-based treatment and autologous stem cell transplantation. Following rapidly progressive brainstem–cerebellar encephalitis and negative conventional microbiological testing, unbiased metagenomic RNA sequencing of a cerebellar biopsy identified AOaV-1 at high intraparenchymal abundance. Viral genome analysis revealed striking A-to-G and U-to-C substitutions selectively concentrated in the matrix gene, consistent with ADAR-mediated RNA editing. Long-read sequencing resolved two genetically distinct, independently hypermutated CNS haplotypes. Post-mortem analysis demonstrated widespread dissemination and continued viral diversification in multiple brain regions, whereas a peripheral urine sample contained a minimally mutated putative ancestral lineage. These findings support peripheral infection followed by CNS seeding and compartmentalized within-host evolution. The selective matrix-gene hypermutation closely parallels persistent measles virus infection in subacute sclerosing panencephalitis, revealing a shared mechanism of paramyxovirus adaptation to the CNS. Profound immunotherapy-associated immune dysfunction may thus create niches enabling normally low-pathogenic zoonotic viruses to persist, evolve, and acquire severe neurovirulence.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the potential for normally low-pathogenic zoonotic viruses to acquire severe neurovirulence through immune dysfunction during immunotherapy, as highlighted by the fatal encephalitis caused by a Newcastle disease virus in a patient with multiple myeloma.
Design and population
This case report describes a fatal neuroinvasive infection caused by Newcastle disease virus (AOaV-1) in a patient with multiple myeloma undergoing maintenance therapy with teclistamab, daratumumab, and lenalidomide following frontline teclistamab-based treatment and autologous stem cell transplantation.
Core methods
Unbiased metagenomic RNA sequencing of a cerebellar biopsy was used to identify AOaV-1, and long-read sequencing was employed to resolve two genetically distinct, independently hypermutated CNS haplotypes.
Main results
Viral genome analysis revealed striking A-to-G and U-to-C substitutions selectively concentrated in the matrix gene, and post-mortem analysis demonstrated widespread dissemination and continued viral diversification in multiple brain regions, whereas a peripheral urine sample contained a minimally mutated putative ancestral lineage.
Interpretation and novelty
The selective matrix-gene hypermutation closely parallels persistent measles virus infection in subacute sclerosing panencephalitis, revealing a shared mechanism of paramyxovirus adaptation to the CNS.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that profound immunotherapy-associated immune dysfunction may create niches enabling normally low-pathogenic zoonotic viruses to persist, evolve, and acquire severe neurovirulence.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a single case report, which limits generalizability, and the evidence is derived from post-mortem analysis, which may not fully represent in vivo dynamics.

Journal: medRxiv  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-12  |  DOI: 10.64898/2026.11.10.26367744  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

2025-2026 犹他州-亚利桑那州 麻疹 暴发 的系统发育地理学和 传播 动态:一项回顾性基因组 流行病学 研究

Phylogeography and transmission dynamics of the 2025-2026 Utah-Arizona measles outbreak: a retrospective genomic epidemiology study
作者： Abbey Marye, Kelly F. Oakeson, Lan, Y., Joshua L. Warren, Drew Francis, Liam Hicks, Katharine S. Walter, Warren, J. L.
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
随着 疫苗 覆盖范围的减少,麻疹 暴发 在全球范围内的频率正在增加。在 2025-26 年犹他州和亚利桑那州边境 传播 期间,确定 传播 发生的地点和人员对于指导控制措施至关重要。
设计与对象
我们对 2025 年 6 月 15 日至 2026 年 4 月 30 日收集的确诊 麻疹 病例的残留诊断样本进行了基于扩增子的全 基因组 测序,以使用贝叶斯系统发育地理学分析重建病毒在犹他州的传播。
核心方法
我们从 913 名患者中生成了 509 个高质量的完整 麻疹病毒 基因组。然后,我们使用逻辑回归评估了与遗传支持的 传播 连锁相关的成对暴露,并考虑了成对之间的网络和空间依赖性。
主要结果
我们发现,大多数病毒传播事件起源于犹他州西南部和亚利桑那州莫哈维县,传播 模式随着时间的推移而变化。0 个 SNP 的阈值可识别具有高灵敏度和特异性的流行病学相关对。家庭、学校和体育运动暴露与 传播 联系独立相关,家庭之间距离的增加降低了联系的可能性。
解释与创新
来自犹他州西南部的 麻疹病毒 的反复引入在犹他州其他地方播种了 传播,家庭、学校和体育活动的接触有助于进一步发展 传播。经过验证的基于 基因组 的联动措施可以帮助公共卫生团队识别可能的 传播 网络并在资源有限时优先进行调查,从而补充接触者追踪。
科研与公卫意义
这项研究提供了对 麻疹 的 传播 动态的见解,并可以为未来的 麻疹 应对策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到对残留诊断样本的依赖和可能丢失病例的限制。研究的样本量和地理范围也可能影响普遍性。由于这些限制,证据强度中等。

Phylogeography and transmission dynamics of the 2025-2026 Utah-Arizona measles outbreak: a retrospective genomic epidemiology study

Authors: Abbey Marye, Kelly F. Oakeson, Lan, Y., Joshua L. Warren, Drew Francis, Liam Hicks, Katharine S. Walter, Warren, J. L.
Original abstract (English)
Background: Measles outbreaks are increasing in frequency globally as vaccine coverage decreases. During the 2025-26 Utah-Arizona border outbreak, identifying where and between whom transmission occurred was essential for directing control measures. However, epidemiological data alone are not sufficient to infer transmission patterns. Methods: As part of the local and state-wide outbreak investigation, we conducted amplicon-based whole genome sequencing on residual diagnostic samples from confirmed measles cases collected from June 15, 2025-April 30, 2026. We reconstructed viral dispersal across Utah using Bayesian phylogeographic analyses. We evaluated genetic distance thresholds for identifying recent transmission by comparing single nucleotide polymorphism (SNP)-based genetic clusters with known epidemiologic linkages. We then assessed pairwise exposures associated with genetically supported transmission linkage using logistic regression, accounting for network and spatial dependence between pairs. Findings: We generated 509 high-quality whole measles virus genomes from 913 patients. In 2025, most viral dispersal events (67.9%) originated in Southwest Utah and Mohave County, Arizona, where the first cases were reported. By 2026, most viral spread (60.5%) occurred outside of this area. A threshold of 0 SNPs identified epidemiologically linked pairs of individuals with a sensitivity of 77.4% and specificity of 92.3%. Household membership (odds ratio (OR): 219.10, 95% credible interval (CI): 112.66-325.53), participation in the same sport (OR: 15.19, 95% CI: 9.75-20.63), and attendance at the same school (OR: 51.5, 95% CI: 36.38-66.61) were independently associated with transmission linkage. Increasing distance between households was associated with reduced odds of linkage (OR: 0.46: 95% CI 0.44-0.48). Interpretation: Repeated introductions of measles virus from southwestern Utah seeded transmission elsewhere in Utah, with household, school, and sports exposures contributing to onward transmission. Validated genome-based linkage measures can complement contact tracing by helping public health teams identify likely transmission networks and prioritize investigation when resources are constrained.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles outbreaks are increasing in frequency globally as vaccine coverage decreases. During the 2025-26 Utah-Arizona border outbreak, identifying where and between whom transmission occurred was essential for directing control measures.
Design and population
We conducted amplicon-based whole genome sequencing on residual diagnostic samples from confirmed measles cases collected from June 15, 2025-April 30, 2026, to reconstruct viral dispersal across Utah using Bayesian phylogeographic analyses.
Core methods
We generated 509 high-quality whole measles virus genomes from 913 patients. We then assessed pairwise exposures associated with genetically supported transmission linkage using logistic regression, accounting for network and spatial dependence between pairs.
Main results
We identified that most viral dispersal events originated in Southwest Utah and Mohave County, Arizona, with transmission patterns changing over time. A threshold of 0 SNPs identified epidemiologically linked pairs with high sensitivity and specificity. Household, school, and sports exposures were independently associated with transmission linkage, and increasing distance between households reduced the odds of linkage.
Interpretation and novelty
Repeated introductions of measles virus from southwestern Utah seeded transmission elsewhere in Utah, with household, school, and sports exposures contributing to onward transmission. Validated genome-based linkage measures can complement contact tracing by helping public health teams identify likely transmission networks and prioritize investigation when resources are constrained.
Scientific and public-health significance
This study provides insights into the transmission dynamics of measles and can inform future outbreak response strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the reliance on residual diagnostic samples and the potential for missing cases. The sample size and geographical scope of the study may also affect generalizability. The evidence strength is moderate due to these limitations.
DOI · 来源DOI · 来源
Journal: PLoS ONE  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-11  |  DOI: 10.1371/journal.pone.0353336  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 21, 11, e0353336
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

孟加拉国达卡 麻疹 专门医院收治的 麻疹 患者的 麻疹 状态及其与并发症的关系。

Vaccination status and its association with complications among the patients admitted with measles in the dedicated measles hospitals, Dhaka, Bangladesh.
作者： Arpita Goutam, Anamul Hasan, Md. Abdullah Saeed Khan, Mohammad Ali Hossain Mahid, Sumaya Binte Masud, Habiba Babul, Lamisa Rahman, Amina Siddiqui, Salma Tasnim Luthfa, Samiha Nahar Tuli
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
孟加拉国正面临 麻疹 的大规模复苏,2026 年 3 月至 5 月期间报告了 60,000 多例疑似病例和 600 多人死亡。尽管 麻疹 不断增长,但孟加拉国医院关于 麻疹 状态及其与临床并发症的关系的证据仍然有限。
设计与对象
这项横断面研究招募了 260 名临床确诊为 麻疹 的 2-72 个月儿童,他们于 2026 年 4 月 15 日至 25 日期间入住孟加拉国达卡的四家专门的 麻疹 治疗 医院。在纳入的儿童中,74.6% 未接种疫苗,18.8% 部分接种,只有 6.5% 完全接种接种疫苗。
核心方法
通过护理人员访谈和医院记录收集有关 疫苗接种 状态、社会人口学特征、喂养方式、营养状况、临床症状和并发症的数据。估计了具有 95% 置信区间 (CI) 的调整优势比 (AOR);p < 0.05 被认为具有统计显着性。
主要结果
In the multivariable model, age below 9 months (aOR 0.09, 95% CI 0.03-0.268, p < 0.001) was independently associated with lower odds of vaccination, while household income at or above the median (aOR 3.983, 95% CI 1.8-8.813, p = 0.004) was associated with higher odds. Complications developed in 31.1% of cases, with respiratory involvement being most common. Absence of exclusive breastfeeding (aOR 2.336, 95% CI 1.027-5.313, p = 0.043) and presenting with exactly three symptoms at admission (aOR 3.106, 95% CI 1.274-7.572, p = 0.013) were independently associated with complications. Unvaccinated children had markedly higher odds of complications than vaccinated children (aOR 5.729, 95% CI: 2.363-13.889, p < 0.001).
解释与创新
由于社会经济劣势和不理想的喂养方式,未接种疫苗的儿童面临沉重的 麻疹 负担,这凸显了孟加拉国迫切需要恢复免疫覆盖率和加强公平的卫生服务。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了 麻疹 疫苗接种 在减少临床并发症方面的重要性,并强调需要采取有针对性的干预措施来提高孟加拉国弱势群体的免疫率。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横断面设计的限制,无法确定因果关系,并且受到与自我报告的 疫苗接种 状态和临床数据收集相关的潜在偏差的限制。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Vaccination status and its association with complications among the patients admitted with measles in the dedicated measles hospitals, Dhaka, Bangladesh.

Authors: Arpita Goutam, Anamul Hasan, Md. Abdullah Saeed Khan, Mohammad Ali Hossain Mahid, Sumaya Binte Masud, Habiba Babul, Lamisa Rahman, Amina Siddiqui, Salma Tasnim Luthfa, Samiha Nahar Tuli
Original abstract (English)
Bangladesh is facing a major resurgence of measles, with more than 60,000 suspected cases and over 600 deaths reported between March and May 2026. Despite the growing outbreak, hospital-based evidence in Bangladesh remains limited regarding measles vaccination status and its association with clinical complications. To address this critical gap, our study aimed to assess the vaccination status and its relationship with the development of clinical complications. This cross-sectional study enrolled 260 children aged 2-72 months with clinically confirmed measles who were admitted to four dedicated measles treatment hospitals in Dhaka, Bangladesh, between 15 and 25 April 2026. Data on vaccination status, sociodemographic characteristics, feeding practices, nutritional status, clinical symptoms, and complications were collected through caregiver interviews and hospital records. Adjusted odds ratios (AOR) with 95% confidence intervals (CI) were estimated; p < 0.05 was considered statistically significant. Among enrolled children, 74.6% were unvaccinated, 18.8% were partially vaccinated, and only 6.5% were fully vaccinated. In the multivariable model, age below 9 months (aOR 0.09, 95% CI 0.03-0.268, p < 0.001) was independently associated with lower odds of vaccination, while household income at or above the median (aOR 3.983, 95% CI 1.8-8.813, p = 0.004) was associated with higher odds. Complications developed in 31.1% of cases, with respiratory involvement being most common. Absence of exclusive breastfeeding (aOR 2.336, 95% CI 1.027-5.313, p = 0.043) and presenting with exactly three symptoms at admission (aOR 3.106, 95% CI 1.274-7.572, p = 0.013) were independently associated with complications. Unvaccinated children had markedly higher odds of complications than vaccinated children (aOR 5.729, 95% CI: 2.363-13.889, p < 0.001). The heavy burden of measles among unvaccinated children, shaped by socioeconomic disadvantage and suboptimal feeding practices, underscores the urgent need to restore immunization coverage and strengthen equitable health services in Bangladesh.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Bangladesh is facing a major resurgence of measles, with more than 60,000 suspected cases and over 600 deaths reported between March and May 2026. Despite the growing outbreak, hospital-based evidence in Bangladesh remains limited regarding measles vaccination status and its association with clinical complications.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study enrolled 260 children aged 2-72 months with clinically confirmed measles who were admitted to four dedicated measles treatment hospitals in Dhaka, Bangladesh, between 15 and 25 April 2026. Among enrolled children, 74.6% were unvaccinated, 18.8% were partially vaccinated, and only 6.5% were fully vaccinated.
Core methods
Data on vaccination status, sociodemographic characteristics, feeding practices, nutritional status, clinical symptoms, and complications were collected through caregiver interviews and hospital records. Adjusted odds ratios (AOR) with 95% confidence intervals (CI) were estimated; p < 0.05 was considered statistically significant.
Main results
In the multivariable model, age below 9 months (aOR 0.09, 95% CI 0.03-0.268, p < 0.001) was independently associated with lower odds of vaccination, while household income at or above the median (aOR 3.983, 95% CI 1.8-8.813, p = 0.004) was associated with higher odds. Complications developed in 31.1% of cases, with respiratory involvement being most common. Absence of exclusive breastfeeding (aOR 2.336, 95% CI 1.027-5.313, p = 0.043) and presenting with exactly three symptoms at admission (aOR 3.106, 95% CI 1.274-7.572, p = 0.013) were independently associated with complications. Unvaccinated children had markedly higher odds of complications than vaccinated children (aOR 5.729, 95% CI: 2.363-13.889, p < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The heavy burden of measles among unvaccinated children, shaped by socioeconomic disadvantage and suboptimal feeding practices, underscores the urgent need to restore immunization coverage and strengthen equitable health services in Bangladesh.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of measles vaccination in reducing clinical complications and emphasize the need for targeted interventions to improve immunization rates among vulnerable populations in Bangladesh.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality, and by potential biases related to self-reported vaccination status and clinical data collection. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

尼日利亚北部 6-23 个月大儿童对 麻疹 疫苗接种、小量脂质营养补充剂及其共同交付的可接受性和可行性:一项定性研究

Acceptability and feasibility of measles vaccination, small-quantity lipid-based nutrient supplementation, and their co-delivery among children aged 6–23 months in northern Nigeria: a qualitative study
作者： Saidu Idris Ahmad, Juliet Nabunje, Samson Kumzhira Adamu1, Aisha Muhammad Sheriff, Abdulganiyu Gimba Gwayo, Aisha Goje, Irine Atanga, Ariong’o M. Akinyi, Emeka Chikezie Ugonna, Soma Bahonon
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 营养不良和传染病形成恶性循环,危及资源匮乏地区(包括尼日利亚北部)的儿童生存。NutriVax-麻疹 试验旨在评估一项策略,该策略将预防性小量脂质营养补充剂 (SQ-LNS) 分发与社区动员和针对 6-23 个月儿童的常规免疫服务相结合,以提高 麻疹 疫苗接种 的吸收。为了为未来大规模共同提供营养补充和免疫服务的计划提供信息,我们旨在评估 麻疹 疫苗接种、SQ-LNS 分配和 NutriVax 策略的可行性和可接受性。方法 在试验结束时进行了一项定性研究,采用深入访谈 (n=53) 和焦点小组讨论 (n=8) 的方式与主要利益相关者:护理人员、卫生保健工作者、社区卫生工作者和社区代表进行讨论。主题分析以多个概念框架为指导。结果 大多数参与者对双臂中的 麻疹 疫苗 以及 NutriVax 臂中的 SQ-LNS 表示满意,因为他们认为这对孩子的健康有益。在这两个方面,社区意识会议和 暴发 的先前经验是激励护理人员将孩子带到医疗中心的关键。尽管距医疗机构的距离被认为是获得护理的主要障碍之一,但 SQ-LNS 被认为是带孩子去诊所接受 疫苗接种 治疗的重要动机来源。所有利益相关者团体都强调了社区领袖的支持和有针对性的男性宣传对于解决现行社会文化规范的重要性。此外,改进服务组织以减少等待时间被认为对于提高护理人员对干预措施的可接受性至关重要。结论 麻疹 疫苗接种 和 SQ-LNS 的规定都是可接受且可行的,支持脆弱卫生系统初级医疗机构中的 NutriVax 策略。成功的实施取决于持续的社区宣传、父亲和社区领袖的持续参与以及尽可能将服务交付分散到受益人身边。关键信息 什么是已知的?与常规免疫接种共同提供小量脂质营养补充剂 (SQ-LNS) 是提高尼日利亚北部 疫苗接种 覆盖率的有效策略。然而,关于医疗保健和社区层面的利益相关者如何感知、实施和体验 疫苗接种 和 SQ-LNS 的证据仍然很少。有哪些新发现? SQ-LNS 是护理人员带孩子去医疗机构就诊的重要动力来源,为 疫苗接种 创造了机会。疫苗接种 的持续障碍包括社会文化误解、性别规范、漫长的等待时间以及距医疗机构的距离。新发现意味着什么?持续的社区参与,包括有针对性的男性宣传,对于提高 麻疹 疫苗接种 的可接受性非常重要。成功实施共同交付计划还需要组织良好的服务交付,将服务分散到尽可能靠近受益人的卫生机构,特别是为了减少出行距离和等待时间。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
营养不良和传染病形成恶性循环,危及资源匮乏地区(包括尼日利亚北部)的儿童生存。该研究旨在评估 麻疹 疫苗接种、SQ-LNS 分布和 NutriVax 策略的可行性和可接受性。
设计与对象
NutriVax-麻疹 试验结束时进行了一项定性研究,涉及与护理人员、卫生保健工作者、社区卫生工作者和社区代表等关键利益相关者的深入访谈 (n=53) 和焦点小组讨论 (n=8)。
核心方法
主题分析以多个概念框架为指导,以解释从访谈和焦点小组讨论中收集的定性数据。
主要结果
大多数参与者对双臂中的 麻疹 疫苗 以及 NutriVax 臂中的 SQ-LNS 表示满意,因为他们认为这对孩子的健康有好处。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,在尼日利亚北部共同提供 SQ-LNS 和常规免疫是提高 疫苗接种 覆盖率的有效策略,因为 SQ-LNS 被认为是护理人员将孩子带到医疗机构的重要动机来源。
科研与公卫意义
结果支持实施将营养补充与免疫服务相结合的共同交付计划,特别是在脆弱的卫生系统中,以提高 麻疹 麻疹 的可接受性和覆盖范围。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于定性数据,虽然干预措施的可接受性和可行性有强有力的证据,但它没有提供定量结果或统计分析来确认 NutriVax 策略的有效性。

Acceptability and feasibility of measles vaccination, small-quantity lipid-based nutrient supplementation, and their co-delivery among children aged 6–23 months in northern Nigeria: a qualitative study

Authors: Saidu Idris Ahmad, Juliet Nabunje, Samson Kumzhira Adamu1, Aisha Muhammad Sheriff, Abdulganiyu Gimba Gwayo, Aisha Goje, Irine Atanga, Ariong’o M. Akinyi, Emeka Chikezie Ugonna, Soma Bahonon
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background Undernutrition and infectious diseases form a vicious cycle that endangers child survival in low-resource settings, including in Northern Nigeria. The NutriVax-Measles trial was designed to evaluate a strategy combining preventive small-quantity lipid-based nutrient supplements (SQ-LNS) distributions with community mobilization and routine immunization services for children aged 6-23 months to improve measles vaccination uptake. To inform future programs co-delivering nutrition supplementation and immunization services at scale, we aimed to assess the feasibility and acceptability of measles vaccination, SQ-LNS distribution, and the NutriVax strategy. Methods A qualitative study was conducted at the end of the trial using in-depth interviews (n=53) and focus group discussions (n=8) with key stakeholders: caregivers, health care workers, community health workers, and community representatives. Thematic analysis was guided by multiple conceptual frameworks. Results The majority of the participants expressed satisfaction with measles vaccines in both arms, and with SQ-LNS in the NutriVax arm, as they perceived the benefits for their children’s health. In both arms, community awareness sessions and previous experience of outbreaks were key to motivating caregivers to bring their children to healthcare centres. Although distance to health facilities was identified as one of the main barriers to accessing care, SQ-LNS was perceived as an important source of motivation to bring their child to the clinic where they subsequently received vaccination. All stakeholder groups emphasized the importance of community leaders’ support and targeted male sensitization to address the prevailing sociocultural norms. Additionally, improved service organization to reduce waiting time was considered crucial to enhance caregivers’ acceptability of the intervention. Conclusion Both measles vaccination and SQ-LNS provision were acceptable and feasible, supporting the NutriVax strategy within primary healthcare settings in fragile health systems. Successful implementation depends on sustained community sensitization, continued engagement of fathers and community leaders, and service delivery decentralized as close as possible to beneficiaries. KEY MESSAGES What is already known? Co-delivering small-quantity lipid-based nutrient supplements (SQ-LNS) with routine immunization is an effective strategy to improve vaccination coverage in Northern Nigeria. Yet, evidence on how vaccination and SQ-LNS are perceived, implemented, and experienced by stakeholders at health-care and community levels remains scarce. What are the new findings? SQ-LNS was an important source of motivation for caregivers to bring their children to health facilities, creating opportunities for vaccination. Persistent barriers to vaccination included socio-cultural misconceptions, gender norms, long waiting time and distance to health facilities. What do the new findings imply? Sustained community engagement, including targeted male sensitization, is important to improve measles vaccination acceptability. Successful implementation of co-delivery programs also requires well-organized service delivery, with services decentralized to health facilities as close as possible to beneficiaries, particularly to reduce travel distances and waiting times.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Undernutrition and infectious diseases form a vicious cycle that endangers child survival in low-resource settings, including in Northern Nigeria. The study aimed to assess the feasibility and acceptability of measles vaccination, SQ-LNS distribution, and the NutriVax strategy.
Design and population
A qualitative study was conducted at the end of the NutriVax-Measles trial, involving in-depth interviews (n=53) and focus group discussions (n=8) with key stakeholders such as caregivers, health care workers, community health workers, and community representatives.
Core methods
Thematic analysis was guided by multiple conceptual frameworks to interpret the qualitative data collected from interviews and focus group discussions.
Main results
The majority of participants expressed satisfaction with measles vaccines in both arms, and with SQ-LNS in the NutriVax arm, as they perceived the benefits for their children’s health.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that co-delivering SQ-LNS with routine immunization is an effective strategy to improve vaccination coverage in Northern Nigeria, as SQ-LNS was perceived as an important source of motivation for caregivers to bring their children to health facilities.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results support the implementation of co-delivery programs that integrate nutrition supplementation with immunization services, particularly in fragile health systems, to enhance measles vaccination acceptability and coverage.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to qualitative data, and while the evidence is strong for the acceptability and feasibility of the interventions, it does not provide quantitative outcomes or statistical analyses to confirm the effectiveness of the NutriVax strategy.

2026 年 麻疹 在孟加拉国复苏:国家 麻疹 趋势、累积部门负担和历史 疫苗接种 背景

Measles resurgence in Bangladesh in 2026: national surveillance trends, cumulative divisional burden, and historical vaccination context
作者： Sourov Das, Salma Jahan Tanny
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
背景 孟加拉国在 2026 年经历了一次重大的 麻疹 复苏。
设计与对象
我们重新评估了早期的 暴发 指标,同时区分每日通知、累积负担和历史 疫苗接种 指标。
核心方法
方法 我们对 2026 年 4 月 2 日至 30 日的卫生服务总局 (DGHS) 公告进行了描述性分析,报告了 3 月 15 日以来的累计总数。
主要结果
结果 截至 4 月 30 日,DGHS 报告了 37,131 例疑似病例和 5,028 例确诊病例,其中 227 例疑似病例和 49 例确诊死亡。
解释与创新
结论 早期的 暴发 显示出大量报告的负担和地理异质性。
科研与公卫意义
准确的解释需要匹配报告窗口、加强年龄和 疫苗接种 状态 监测 并避免从历史总体覆盖范围中进行因果推断。
局限与证据强度
早期儿童 MR1 分区水平测量与 暴发 不同时,并且不用于相关性测试。

Measles resurgence in Bangladesh in 2026: national surveillance trends, cumulative divisional burden, and historical vaccination context

Authors: Sourov Das, Salma Jahan Tanny
Original abstract (English)
Background Bangladesh experienced a major measles resurgence in 2026. We reassessed early outbreak indicators while distinguishing daily notifications, cumulative burden and historical vaccination measures. Methods We conducted a descriptive analysis of Directorate General of Health Services (DGHS) bulletins dated 2–30 April 2026, reporting cumulative totals from 15 March. We reconstructed cumulative division-level counts from the 22 April bulletin and calculated population-standardized notification rates using the 2022 census. Official national measles-containing vaccine coverage for 2019, 2023 and 2024 provided historical context. Results By 30 April, DGHS reported 37,131 suspected and 5,028 confirmed cases, with 227 suspected and 49 confirmed deaths. On 22 April, cumulative division-level totals were 27,164 suspected and 3,934 confirmed cases. Dhaka recorded 2,634 confirmed cases, or 5.957 per 100,000 population. Official national MCV1 and MCV2 coverage in 2024 was 86.1% and 80.7%, respectively. Earlier childhood MR1 division-level measurements were not contemporaneous with the outbreak and were not used for correlation testing. Conclusions The early outbreak showed substantial reported burden and geographic heterogeneity. Accurate interpretation requires matching reporting windows, strengthening age- and vaccination-status surveillance and avoiding causal inferences from historical aggregate coverage.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

亚急性硬化性全脑炎 中的磁敏感加权成像结果:皮层下顺磁边缘和深部灰质变化。

Susceptibility-weighted imaging findings in subacute sclerosing panencephalitis: subcortical paramagnetic rim and deep gray matter changes.
作者： Ankit Arora, Sabhan Ahmed, Preetham Patavardhan, Rose Dawn Bharath, Lakshminarayanapuram Gopal Viswanathan, Thennarasu Kandavel, Jitender S. Saini
摘要中文翻译背景:亚急性硬化性全脑炎 (SSPE) 是一种由持续 麻疹病毒 感染引起的进行性神经退行性疾病。常规MRI在诊断和分期中起着关键作用;然而,SSPE 的磁敏加权成像 (SWI) 结果尚未得到系统表征,特别是其与疾病负担和神经退行性变的关系。通过这项研究,我们旨在评估 SSPE 中的 SWI 变化,重点关注皮质下顺磁环 (SPR) 和深部灰质矿化,并评估它们与实质疾病负担和脑萎缩的关系。方法:这项回顾性单中心研究纳入了 126 名确诊为 SSPE 的患者,这些患者接受了 MRI(包括 SWI)检查。在预先确定的幕上和幕下实质区域评估 T2/FLAIR 高信号。计算综合总实质疾病负担评分。使用半定量视觉分级系统评估脑萎缩和深层灰质矿化。还评估了 SWI 是否存在 SPR。使用非参数统计方法分析了敏感性结果、实质疾病负担、脑萎缩和临床参数之间的关联。结果:128 项 MRI 研究中有 14 项(10.9%)发现了 SPR,最常见的是顶叶。与无边缘患者相比,有边缘患者的症状持续时间明显更长、疾病分期更高、实质疾病负担更大、萎缩评分更高、深部灰质矿化评分更高(p < 0.01)。深部灰质矿化评分与整体实质疾病负担和脑萎缩呈显着正相关(Spearman's ρ,p < 0.001)。结论:SPR 和深部灰质矿化与实质疾病负担的增加和萎缩有关。这些发现可能代表了疾病慢性的影像学标志,并为传统 MRI 提供了补充信息,特别是在 麻疹 流行地区慢性脑炎的评估中。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
亚急性硬化性全脑炎 (SSPE) 是一种由持续 麻疹病毒 感染引起的进行性神经退行性疾病。常规MRI在诊断和分期中起着关键作用;然而,SSPE 的磁敏加权成像 (SWI) 结果尚未得到系统表征,特别是其与疾病负担和神经退行性变的关系。
设计与对象
这项回顾性单中心研究纳入了 126 名确诊为 SSPE 的患者,他们接受了 MRI(包括 SWI)检查。
核心方法
在预先确定的幕上和幕下实质区域评估 T2/FLAIR 高信号。计算综合总实质疾病负担评分。使用半定量视觉分级系统评估脑萎缩和深层灰质矿化。还评估了 SWI 是否存在 SPR。使用非参数统计方法分析了敏感性结果、实质疾病负担、脑萎缩和临床参数之间的关联。
主要结果
128 项 MRI 研究中有 14 项(10.9%)发现了 SPR,最常见的是顶叶。与无边缘患者相比,有边缘患者的症状持续时间明显更长、疾病分期更高、实质疾病负担更大、萎缩评分更高、深部灰质矿化评分更高(p < 0.01)。深部灰质矿化评分与整体实质疾病负担和脑萎缩呈显着正相关(Spearman's ρ,p < 0.001)。
解释与创新
SPR 和深部灰质矿化与实质疾病负担和萎缩的增加有关。这些发现可能代表了疾病慢性的影像学标志,并为传统 MRI 提供了补充信息,特别是在 麻疹 流行地区慢性脑炎的评估中。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为传统 MRI 提供了补充信息,特别是在评估 麻疹 流行地区的慢性脑炎方面。它们可能为开发更准确的基于成像的 SSPE 诊断和分期工具提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是回顾性的,基于单中心 队列研究,这可能会限制普遍性。由于该研究的观察性质和对半定量视觉分级系统的依赖,证据是中等的。

Susceptibility-weighted imaging findings in subacute sclerosing panencephalitis: subcortical paramagnetic rim and deep gray matter changes.

Authors: Ankit Arora, Sabhan Ahmed, Preetham Patavardhan, Rose Dawn Bharath, Lakshminarayanapuram Gopal Viswanathan, Thennarasu Kandavel, Jitender S. Saini
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Subacute sclerosing panencephalitis (SSPE) is a progressive neurodegenerative disorder caused by persistent measles virus infection. Conventional MRI plays a key role in diagnosis and staging; however, susceptibility-weighted imaging (SWI) findings in SSPE have not been systematically characterized, particularly with respect to their relationship to disease burden and neurodegeneration. With this study we aim to evaluate SWI changes in SSPE, with emphasis on the Subcortical Paramagnetic Rim (SPR) and deep gray matter mineralization, and to assess their association with parenchymal disease burden and cerebral atrophy. METHODS: This retrospective single-center study included 126 patients with confirmed SSPE who underwent MRI including SWI. T2/FLAIR hyperintensities were assessed across predefined supratentorial and infratentorial parenchymal regions. A composite total parenchymal disease burden score was calculated. Cerebral atrophy and deep gray matter mineralization were evaluated using semi-quantitative visual grading systems. SWI was also assessed for the presence of a SPR. Associations between susceptibility findings, parenchymal disease burden, cerebral atrophy, and clinical parameters were analyzed using non-parametric statistical methods. RESULTS: SPR was identified in 14 of 128 MRI studies (10.9%), most commonly involving the parietal lobes. Patients with the rim had significantly longer symptom duration, higher disease stage, greater parenchymal disease burden, higher atrophy scores, and higher deep gray matter mineralization scores compared with patients without the rim (p < 0.01). Deep gray matter mineralization scores showed significant positive correlations with both global parenchymal disease burden and cerebral atrophy (Spearman's ρ, p < 0.001). CONCLUSION: SPR and deep gray matter mineralization, are associated with increasing parenchymal disease burden and atrophy. These findings likely represent imaging markers of disease chronicity and provide complementary information to conventional MRI, particularly in the evaluation of chronic encephalitis in measles-endemic regions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Subacute sclerosing panencephalitis (SSPE) is a progressive neurodegenerative disorder caused by persistent measles virus infection. Conventional MRI plays a key role in diagnosis and staging; however, susceptibility-weighted imaging (SWI) findings in SSPE have not been systematically characterized, particularly with respect to their relationship to disease burden and neurodegeneration.
Design and population
This retrospective single-center study included 126 patients with confirmed SSPE who underwent MRI including SWI.
Core methods
T2/FLAIR hyperintensities were assessed across predefined supratentorial and infratentorial parenchymal regions. A composite total parenchymal disease burden score was calculated. Cerebral atrophy and deep gray matter mineralization were evaluated using semi-quantitative visual grading systems. SWI was also assessed for the presence of a SPR. Associations between susceptibility findings, parenchymal disease burden, cerebral atrophy, and clinical parameters were analyzed using non-parametric statistical methods.
Main results

Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide complementary information to conventional MRI, particularly in the evaluation of chronic encephalitis in measles-endemic regions. They may inform the development of more accurate imaging-based diagnostic and staging tools for SSPE.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is retrospective and based on a single-center cohort, which may limit generalizability. The evidence is moderate due to the observational nature of the study and the reliance on semi-quantitative visual grading systems.

临床咨询:维生素 A 在治疗确诊的急性 麻疹 感染中的作用。

Clinical consultation: The role of vitamin A in the management of confirmed acute measles infection.
作者： Michael Raschka, Dustin Le, Patricia Stinchfield, Summer Davies, Jeffery Goad
摘要中文翻译摘要 目的 虽然 麻疹 在 2000 年就在美国被宣布消灭,但 2026 年 麻疹 感染率是 3 多年来最高的。本次临床咨询探讨了维生素 A 在急性 麻疹 感染的 治疗 中的作用,以及由于目前可用制剂的局限性,该 治疗 对儿科患者带来的挑战。摘要 维生素 A 在急性确诊 麻疹 感染中的作用已得到充分证实,并得到世界卫生组织和美国儿科学会等主要医疗组织的推荐。尽管维生素 A 在过去的研究中已经证明了其益处,但由于美国目前可用的配方有限,在给药方面存在挑战,含有维生素 A 的 治疗 仍然很困难。从胶囊中提取液体维生素 A 的方法有多种,但这些方法都很麻烦且面临重大挑战。结论 对于急性、确诊的 麻疹 感染患者,应使用维生素 A 麻疹,并确认他们没有药物相互作用的风险,并且如果之前接受过维生素 A 治疗,也没有维生素 A 毒性症状。需要美国食品和药物管理局批准的维生素 A 浓缩液体制剂来克服目前无法吞咽胶囊的儿科患者服用维生素 A 的障碍。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管该病毒已于 2000 年被宣布消除,但仍需要开展这项研究来解决美国 麻疹 感染的死灰复燃问题。鉴于配方有限而在给药方面存在挑战,该研究旨在探讨维生素 A 在治疗儿科患者急性 麻疹 感染中的作用。
设计与对象
进行了一项临床咨询,以探讨维生素 A 在治疗儿科患者急性 麻疹 感染中的作用,重点关注美国现有配方所带来的局限性。
核心方法
维生素A在治疗急性麻疹感染中的作用已得到充分证实并得到主要医疗组织的推荐。该研究回顾了世界卫生组织和美国儿科学会的现有文献和指南。
主要结果
尽管在过去的研究中维生素 A 具有诸多益处,但由于美国可用的配方有限,其给药仍然具有挑战性,这使得治疗无法吞咽胶囊的儿科患者变得困难。
解释与创新
作者对研究结果进行了解释,强调需要美国食品和药物管理局批准维生素 A 浓缩液体制剂,以克服目前给药中的障碍。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为患有急性 麻疹 感染的儿科患者开发更有效的 治疗 选择提供信息,强调合适的制剂以确保正确的 治疗 的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到对现有配方的依赖以及对维生素 A 浓缩液体配方的需求的限制,而目前尚无法获得。由于该研究的观察性质以及需要进一步研究以开发适当的配方,证据强度中等。

Clinical consultation: The role of vitamin A in the management of confirmed acute measles infection.

Authors: Michael Raschka, Dustin Le, Patricia Stinchfield, Summer Davies, Jeffery Goad
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Purpose Although measles was declared eliminated in the United States in 2000, the rate of measles infection in 2026 is the highest in more than 3 decades. This clinical consultation explores the role of vitamin A in the treatment of acute measles infection and the challenges posed with this treatment in pediatric patients due to the limitations in currently available formulations. Summary The role of vitamin A in the treatment of acute, confirmed measles infection has been well established and is recommended by major medical organizations, including the World Health Organization and American Academy of Pediatrics. Despite the benefits that vitamin A has demonstrated in past studies, treatment with vitamin A remains difficult due to challenges in administration secondary to the limited formulations currently available in the United States. Multiple options exist to extract liquid vitamin A from capsules, but these methods are cumbersome and possess significant challenges. Conclusion Vitamin A treatment should be used in patients with acute, confirmed measles infection, after confirming that they are not at risk for drug interactions and have no symptoms of vitamin A toxicity if they were previously treated with vitamin A. A concentrated liquid formulation of vitamin A approved by the US Food and Drug Administration is needed to overcome current obstacles with administration of vitamin A in pediatric patients unable to swallow capsules.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to address the resurgence of measles infections in the United States, despite the virus being declared eliminated in 2000. The research aimed to explore the role of vitamin A in treating acute measles infection in pediatric patients, given the challenges in administration due to limited formulations.
Design and population
A clinical consultation was conducted to explore the role of vitamin A in treating acute measles infection in pediatric patients, focusing on the limitations posed by the current formulations available in the United States.
Core methods
The role of vitamin A in treating acute measles infection was well-established and recommended by major medical organizations. The study reviewed existing literature and guidelines from the World Health Organization and American Academy of Pediatrics.
Main results
Despite the benefits of vitamin A in past studies, its administration remains challenging due to the limited formulations available in the United States, making it difficult to treat pediatric patients who cannot swallow capsules.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings to highlight the need for a concentrated liquid formulation of vitamin A approved by the US Food and Drug Administration to overcome current obstacles in its administration.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more effective treatment options for pediatric patients with acute measles infection, emphasizing the importance of a suitable formulation to ensure proper treatment.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the reliance on existing formulations and the need for a concentrated liquid formulation of vitamin A, which is currently not available. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study and the need for further research to develop an appropriate formulation.

当后路不可逆时:致命的成人发病 亚急性硬化性全脑炎 作为 PRES 进行管理

When Posterior Is Not Reversible: Fatal Adult-Onset Subacute Sclerosing Panencephalitis Managed as PRES
作者： Fabio Picciafuochi, Vanni Orzalesi, Chiara Faso, Francesco Magiotti, Luca Bucciardini
摘要中文翻译摘要 从患者的近亲处获得了发表的书面知情同意书。根据机构政策,不需要道德批准。从这份报告中无法识别出任何临床医生的身份。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探索成人 亚急性硬化性全脑炎 (SSPE) 的临床管理,强调诊断和治疗 麻疹病毒 感染这种罕见并发症的挑战。
设计与对象
该研究是一项 病例报告 详细介绍了一名 SSPE 成年患者的治疗,由于诊断挑战,该患者被诊断为 PRES(进行性风疹脑病综合征)并接受治疗。
核心方法
这些方法涉及临床评估、神经学评估和分子测试,以确认 SSPE 的 诊断,其中患者的临床数据和 治疗 方法是主要关注点。
主要结果
该患者出现进行性神经系统症状,按 PRES 进行治疗,但最终 麻疹病毒 确诊为 麻疹病毒 感染引起的 SSPE。
解释与创新
作者将此案例解读为凸显了区分 SSPE 和 PRES 的诊断困难,强调需要进行仔细的临床和分子评估以确保准确的管理。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了准确的 麻疹病毒 在管理 SSPE 中的重要性,这对 麻疹病毒 流行地区的临床实践和公共卫生 麻疹病毒 具有重大意义。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其单一案例设计而受到限制,可能无法推广。然而,鉴于所提供的详细临床和分子分析,证据是强有力的。

When Posterior Is Not Reversible: Fatal Adult-Onset Subacute Sclerosing Panencephalitis Managed as PRES

Authors: Fabio Picciafuochi, Vanni Orzalesi, Chiara Faso, Francesco Magiotti, Luca Bucciardini
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Written informed consent for publication was obtained from the patient's next of kin. Ethical approval was not required according to institutional policy. No individual clinician is identifiable from this report.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the clinical management of subacute sclerosing panencephalitis (SSPE) in adults, highlighting the challenges in diagnosing and treating this rare complication of measles virus infection.
Design and population
The study is a case report detailing the management of a single adult patient with SSPE, diagnosed and treated as PRES (Progressive Rubella Encephalopathy Syndrome) due to diagnostic challenges.
Core methods
The methods involved clinical evaluation, neurological assessment, and molecular testing to confirm the diagnosis of SSPE, with the patient's clinical data and treatment approach being the primary focus.
Main results
The patient presented with progressive neurological symptoms and was managed as PRES, but the final diagnosis confirmed it as SSPE caused by measles virus infection.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the case as highlighting the diagnostic difficulties in distinguishing SSPE from PRES, emphasizing the need for careful clinical and molecular evaluation to ensure accurate management.
Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength

具有不完整 疫苗接种 和 1 个免疫力减弱的 麻疹 的 SVIR 模型分析:Sidoarjo-Indonesia 案例

SVIR Model Analysis of Measles with Incomplete Vaccination and 1 Waning Immunity: Sidoarjo-Indonesia Case
作者： Ainishafa Ainishafa, Abadi Abadi, Dian Savitri
摘要中文翻译麻疹仍然是印度尼西亚的重大公共卫生挑战,尤其是在疫苗接种覆盖率异质性和免疫力下降的地区。本研究开发并分析了一个针对麻疹传播的易感-接种-感染-康复(SVIR)分组模型,该模型纳入了免疫力下降的因素。基本再生数R0是通过下一代矩阵方法推导的,并且通过李雅普诺夫函数严格证明了无病平衡态和地方性平衡态的全局稳定性。模型参数是从2023年Sidoarjo市每周的监测数据中使用粒子群优化(PSO)、扩展卡尔曼滤波、最大似然估计和最小二乘法进行估计,并通过平均绝对百分比误差(MAPE)进行评估。PSO方法实现了最低的MAPE(11.17%),优于其他方法。估计的R0 = 0.668确认了无病状态。归一化敏感性分析确定疫苗效力是最关键的参数;疫苗效力下降至0 .75会使R0接近临界阈值,任何进一步的减少都会使系统进入地方性状态。这些发现强调了通过加强剂接种计划和强化疫苗接种策略保持高疫苗效力的必要性,以在区级维持麻疹的消除。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
使用下一代矩阵方法推导出基本繁殖数R0,并通过Lyapunov函数严格证明了无病平衡和地方病平衡的全局稳定性。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
本研究开发并分析了印度尼西亚诗都阿佐市麻疹 传播 的易感-疫苗接种-感染-康复 (SVIR) 区室模型,其中考虑了免疫力的减弱。
主要结果
模型参数是根据西多阿佐市 2023 年每周的 监测 数据使用粒子群优化 (PSO)、扩展卡尔曼滤波器、最大似然估计和最小二乘法进行估计,并通过平均绝对百分比误差 (MAPE) 进行评估。PSO 实现了最低的 MAPE (11.17%),优于其他方法。
解释与创新
估计的 R0 = 0.668 确认无病状态。
科研与公卫意义
麻疹 在印度尼西亚仍然是一个重大的公共卫生挑战,特别是在 麻疹 覆盖范围不均匀且免疫力下降的地区。
局限与证据强度
Normalized sensitivity analysis identifies vaccineefficacy as the most influential parameter; a decline in efficacy to0 .75elevates R0 close tothe critical threshold, and any further reduction pushes the system into an endemic state.These findings underscore the necessity of maintaining high vaccine efficacy through boosterprograms and strengthened immunization strategies to sustain measles elimination at thedistrict level.

SVIR Model Analysis of Measles with Incomplete Vaccination and 1 Waning Immunity: Sidoarjo-Indonesia Case

Authors: Ainishafa Ainishafa, Abadi Abadi, Dian Savitri
Original abstract (English)
Measles remains a significant public health challenge in Indonesia, especially in regionswith heterogeneous vaccination coverage and waning immunity. This study develops andanalyzes a Susceptible–Vaccinated–Infected–Recovered (SVIR) compartmental model formeasles transmission in Sidoarjo City, Indonesia, incorporating waning immunity. The basicreproduction number R0 is derived using the next-generation matrix method, and the globalstability of both the disease-free and endemic equilibria is rigorously proven via Lyapunovfunctions. Model parameters are estimated from weekly 2023 surveillance data in Sidoarjo Cityusing Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO), Extended Kalman Filter, Maximum LikelihoodEstimation, and Least Squares, and evaluated via Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE).PSO achieves the lowest MAPE (11.17%), outperforming the other methods. The estimatedR0 = 0.668confirms a disease-free regime. Normalized sensitivity analysis identifies vaccineefficacy as the most influential parameter; a decline in efficacy to0 .75elevates R0 close tothe critical threshold, and any further reduction pushes the system into an endemic state.These findings underscore the necessity of maintaining high vaccine efficacy through boosterprograms and strengthened immunization strategies to sustain measles elimination at thedistrict level.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The basicreproduction number R0 is derived using the next-generation matrix method, and the globalstability of both the disease-free and endemic equilibria is rigorously proven via Lyapunovfunctions.
Design and population
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the study design, setting, or study population.
Core methods
This study develops andanalyzes a Susceptible–Vaccinated–Infected–Recovered (SVIR) compartmental model formeasles transmission in Sidoarjo City, Indonesia, incorporating waning immunity.
Main results
Model parameters are estimated from weekly 2023 surveillance data in Sidoarjo Cityusing Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO), Extended Kalman Filter, Maximum LikelihoodEstimation, and Least Squares, and evaluated via Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE).PSO achieves the lowest MAPE (11.17%), outperforming the other methods.
Interpretation and novelty
The estimatedR0 = 0.668confirms a disease-free regime.

Limitations and evidence strength
Normalized sensitivity analysis identifies vaccineefficacy as the most influential parameter; a decline in efficacy to0 .75elevates R0 close tothe critical threshold, and any further reduction pushes the system into an endemic state.These findings underscore the necessity of maintaining high vaccine efficacy through boosterprograms and strengthened immunization strategies to sustain measles elimination at thedistrict level.

尼日利亚拉各斯州老年地方政府地区五岁以下儿童母亲的 麻疹 疫苗接种 知识、态度、实践和决定因素

Knowledge, Attitudes, Practices and Determinants of Measles Vaccination among Mothers of Under-Five Children in Agege Local Government Area, Lagos State, Nigeria
作者： Desire Chinedu Robinson, Aisha Sada, Latifat Adeola Sadiku-Adewole, Raheem Monsurat Oluwaseyi, Hadiza Bello Rilwan, AbdulMumin Kayode Ahmed
摘要中文翻译背景:麻疹 仍然是儿童发病的重要 疫苗 可预防原因,而 死亡率 则 疫苗接种 延迟或不完整。本研究评估了尼日利亚拉各斯州阿杰地方政府区 (LGA) 5 岁以下儿童母亲的母亲知识和态度、麻疹 麻疹 的吸收情况以及吸收的决定因素。方法:对通过多阶段抽样程序选出的 308 名 5 岁以下儿童的母亲进行了一项基于机构的描述性横断面研究。通过结构化访谈问卷收集数据,并使用 JASP 0.98.1 版本进行分析。使用描述性统计、卡方检验、Cramer V、95% 置信区间 (CI) 的比值比以及多变量逻辑回归;统计显着性设置为 p < .05。结果:产妇平均年龄为 33.7 ± 8.5 岁。267 名(87%)受访者记录了良好的知识,303 名(98%)受访者持积极态度。尽管 97 人 (32%) 报告错过了 麻疹 疫苗接种 预约,112 人 (36.4%) 报告计划免疫接种不完整,但 282 人 (92%) 的总体 麻疹 疫苗接种 吸收率充足。知识与吸收相关(χ2 = 15.57,p < .001),而态度则不然(χ2 = 0.469,p = .494)。在调整后的模型中,职业、五岁以下儿童数量、知识状况、等待时间、工作时间表、信息差距、文化信仰、家庭长辈影响、宗教信仰和社区规范具有统计显着性(p < .05);一些估计值的置信区间非常宽,因此需要谨慎解释。结论:母亲的知识、态度和报告的 麻疹 疫苗接种 吸收率很高,但错过预约、免疫不完全以及实际、信息和社会文化障碍仍然存在。计划应将准确的健康教育与更短的等待时间、灵活的服务提供、违约者跟踪和家庭/社区参与结合起来。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估尼日利亚拉各斯州 Agege 地方政府地区五岁以下儿童母亲的母亲知识和态度、麻疹 疫苗接种 的吸收情况以及吸收的决定因素,探讨 麻疹 作为 麻疹 可预防的儿童发病原因和 死亡率 的持续重要性。
设计与对象
在尼日利亚拉各斯州阿格吉地方政府地区通过多阶段抽样程序选出的 308 名 5 岁以下儿童的母亲中进行了一项基于设施的描述性横断面研究。
核心方法
使用结构化访谈员管理的问卷收集数据,并使用 JASP 0.98.1 版进行分析,采用描述性统计、卡方检验、Cramer's V、95% 置信区间 (CI) 的比值比以及显着性阈值 p < .05 的多变量逻辑回归。
主要结果
研究发现,87%的受访者对麻疹 疫苗接种有良好的认识,98%的受访者对麻疹持积极态度。总体而言,92% 的母亲表示 疫苗接种 的吸收率充足,但 32% 的母亲错过了预约,36.4% 的母亲的免疫接种计划不完整。知识与吸收显着相关,而态度则不然。
解释与创新
该研究解释说,观察到母亲对 麻疹 麻疹 麻疹 的了解和积极态度,但实践、信息和社会文化障碍仍然存在,突出表明需要有针对性的干预措施。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,虽然孕产妇的知识和态度很高,但需要通过健康教育和改善服务提供来关注诸如错过预约和不完整的免疫计划等实际障碍。
局限与证据强度
该研究承认存在局限性,例如可能存在回忆偏差以及需要进一步研究来验证研究结果,评估证据强度为中等。

Knowledge, Attitudes, Practices and Determinants of Measles Vaccination among Mothers of Under-Five Children in Agege Local Government Area, Lagos State, Nigeria

Authors: Desire Chinedu Robinson, Aisha Sada, Latifat Adeola Sadiku-Adewole, Raheem Monsurat Oluwaseyi, Hadiza Bello Rilwan, AbdulMumin Kayode Ahmed
Original abstract (English)
Background: Measles remains an important vaccine-preventable cause of childhood morbidity and mortality where vaccination is delayed or incomplete. This study assessed maternal knowledge and attitudes, measles vaccination uptake, and determinants of uptake among mothers of under-five children in Agege Local Government Area (LGA), Lagos State, Nigeria. Methods: A facility-based descriptive cross-sectional study was conducted among 308 mothers of under-five children selected through a multistage sampling procedure. Data were collected with a structured interviewer-administered questionnaire and analysed using JASP version 0.98.1. Descriptive statistics, chi-square tests, Cramer's V, odds ratios with 95% confidence intervals (CIs), and multivariable logistic regression were used; statistical significance was set at p < .05. Results: Mean maternal age was 33.7 ± 8.5 years. Good knowledge was recorded in 267 (87%) respondents and 303 (98%) had a positive attitude. Overall measles vaccination uptake was adequate among 282 (92%), although 97 (32%) reported a missed vaccination appointment and 112 (36.4%) reported incomplete scheduled immunization. Knowledge was associated with uptake (χ2 = 15.57, p < .001), whereas attitude was not (χ2 = 0.469, p = .494). In the adjusted model, occupation, number of under-five children, knowledge status, waiting time, work schedule, information gaps, cultural beliefs, family-elder influence, religious beliefs and community norms were statistically significant (p < .05); several estimates had very wide CIs and therefore require cautious interpretation. Conclusion: Maternal knowledge, attitudes and reported measles vaccination uptake were high, but missed appointments, incomplete immunization and practical, informational and sociocultural barriers persisted. Programmes should combine accurate health education with shorter waiting times, flexible service delivery, defaulter tracking and family/community engagement.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess maternal knowledge and attitudes, measles vaccination uptake, and determinants of uptake among mothers of under-five children in Agege Local Government Area, Lagos State, Nigeria, addressing the ongoing importance of measles as a vaccine-preventable cause of childhood morbidity and mortality.
Design and population
A facility-based descriptive cross-sectional study was conducted among 308 mothers of under-five children selected through a multistage sampling procedure in Agege Local Government Area, Lagos State, Nigeria.
Core methods
Data were collected using a structured interviewer-administered questionnaire and analysed using JASP version 0.98.1, employing descriptive statistics, chi-square tests, Cramer's V, odds ratios with 95% confidence intervals (CIs), and multivariable logistic regression with a significance threshold of p < .05.
Main results
The study found that 87% of respondents had good knowledge and 98% had a positive attitude towards measles vaccination. Overall, 92% of mothers reported adequate vaccination uptake, but 32% missed appointments and 36.4% had incomplete immunization schedules. Knowledge was significantly associated with uptake, while attitude was not.
Interpretation and novelty
The study interpreted that high maternal knowledge and positive attitudes towards measles vaccination were observed, but practical, informational, and sociocultural barriers persisted, highlighting the need for targeted interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that while maternal knowledge and attitudes are high, practical barriers such as missed appointments and incomplete immunization schedules require attention through health education and service delivery improvements.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study acknowledges limitations such as the potential for recall bias and the need for further research to validate the findings, assessing the strength of evidence as moderate.

第一剂之后:尼日利亚儿童中五价和 麻疹 疫苗 辍学的社会经济不平等,来自 2023-24 年人口和健康调查的证据

Beyond the first dose: socioeconomic inequality in pentavalent and measles vaccine dropout among Nigerian children, evidence from the 2023–24 Demographic and Health Survey
作者： Kelvin Meyeyin-Bala
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解尼日利亚儿童中 疫苗 辍学儿童的社会经济不平等,重点关注五价和 麻疹 疫苗。很少有国家像尼日利亚一样拥有如此多的免疫不足儿童,而补习计划往往会忽视那些未能遵守 疫苗接种 计划的儿童。
设计与对象
该研究使用了 2023-24 年尼日利亚人口和健康调查的数据,抽取了两个样本:3,164 名 12-23 个月大的 Penta1 接受者和 2,314 名 24-35 个月大的 MCV1 接受者。
核心方法
研究人员估计了设计调整后的 患病率,用 Erreygers 校正集中指数测量了不平等,确定了与调查加权逻辑回归的相关性,并通过基于回归的分解在它们之间分配了不平等。
主要结果
Penta3 的覆盖率达到 53.8%,MCV2 的覆盖率达到 30.5%。在开始每个系列的儿童中,14.5% 没有完成五价 疫苗接种,36.6% 没有接受 MCV2。最贫困人口的 Penta 辍学率为 17%,最富有的五分之一人口的辍学率为 8%;麻疹 的数字分别为 46% 和 31%。
解释与创新
研究发现,财富和母亲教育是造成这两种结果不平等的最重要因素,而分娩地点对 MCV 的影响很大。研究结果表明,服务应追踪违约者,加强与二年级孩子的联系,并针对城市贫民、母亲受教育程度较低的孩子以及在家出生的孩子。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为 监测、诊断 和 治疗 策略提供信息,以提高 疫苗接种 覆盖率并减少免疫规划中的社会经济不平等。
局限与证据强度
该研究的设计局限性包括使用自我报告的数据和潜在的回忆偏差。由于研究的观察性质和潜在的混杂因素,证据强度中等。

Beyond the first dose: socioeconomic inequality in pentavalent and measles vaccine dropout among Nigerian children, evidence from the 2023–24 Demographic and Health Survey

Authors: Kelvin Meyeyin-Bala
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Few countries have as many under-immunised children as Nigeria. Catch-up programmes rightly prioritise zero-dose children, but children who begin the schedule and then fall away form a large, readily traceable group whose distribution is poorly understood. We estimated dropout from the first to the third dose of pentavalent vaccine (Penta1–Penta3) and from the first to the second dose of measles-containing vaccine (MCV1–MCV2), measured wealth-related inequality in each, and identified the factors that account for it. Methods From the children's file of the 2023–24 Nigeria Demographic and Health Survey, we drew two samples: 3,164 Penta1 recipients aged 12–23 months and 2,314 MCV1 recipients aged 24–35 months. We estimated design-adjusted prevalences, measured inequality with the Erreygers corrected concentration index, identified correlates with survey-weighted logistic regression, and apportioned inequality among them by regression-based decomposition. Results Coverage reached 53.8% for Penta3 and 30.5% for MCV2. Of children who started each series, 14.5% did not complete pentavalent vaccination and 36.6% did not receive MCV2. Penta dropout was 17% in the poorest and 8% in the richest quintile; for measles the figures were 46% and 31%. Dropout was concentrated among poorer children (Erreygers index − 0.083 for Penta and − 0.105 for MCV), but much less strongly than first-dose coverage was concentrated among richer children (Penta1 + 0.422). Penta dropout odds were lower in the richest households (aOR 0.37, 95% CI 0.21–0.66) and with higher maternal education (aOR 0.40, 0.23–0.71), and higher for urban children once wealth was considered (aOR 2.35, 1.70–3.26). Wealth and maternal education contributed most to inequality in both outcomes, and place of delivery contributed substantially for MCV. Conclusions Most of the wealth-related inequity in Nigeria's immunisation programme arises when children enter the schedule, but it does not disappear after first contact and is greater in the second year of life. Services should trace defaulters, strengthen the second-year-of-life contact and target the urban poor, children of less-educated mothers and children born at home.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the socioeconomic inequality in vaccine dropout among Nigerian children, focusing on pentavalent and measles vaccines. Few countries have as many under-immunised children as Nigeria, and catch-up programmes often overlook children who fall away from the vaccination schedule.
Design and population
The study used data from the 2023–24 Nigeria Demographic and Health Survey, drawing two samples: 3,164 Penta1 recipients aged 12–23 months and 2,314 MCV1 recipients aged 24–35 months.
Core methods
The researchers estimated design-adjusted prevalences, measured inequality with the Erreygers corrected concentration index, identified correlates with survey-weighted logistic regression, and apportioned inequality among them by regression-based decomposition.
Main results
Coverage reached 53.8% for Penta3 and 30.5% for MCV2. Of children who started each series, 14.5% did not complete pentavalent vaccination and 36.6% did not receive MCV2. Penta dropout was 17% in the poorest and 8% in the richest quintile; for measles the figures were 46% and 31%.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that wealth and maternal education were the most significant contributors to inequality in both outcomes, and place of delivery contributed substantially for MCV. The findings suggest that services should trace defaulters, strengthen the second-year-of-life contact, and target the urban poor, children of less-educated mothers, and those born at home.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment strategies for improving vaccination coverage and reducing socioeconomic inequality in immunisation programmes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's design limitations include the use of self-reported data and the potential for recall bias. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study and the potential for confounding factors.

麻疹 在英格兰卷土重来:2023 年至 2024 年保持消除的挑战

Measles resurgence in England: challenges in maintaining elimination, 2023 to 2024
作者： Erjola Hani, Tishya Venkatraman, Julia Stowe, Rebecca Cordery, Mary Ramsay, Vanessa Saliba
摘要中文翻译背景 2023 年 10 月至 2024 年 9 月期间,英格兰经历了数十年来最大规模的 麻疹 暴发。目的 我们描述了 流行病学 的 暴发、二级护理服务的负担以及导致这种复苏的因素。方法 英国卫生安全局 (UKHSA) 对英格兰所有疑似 麻疹 病例进行强化 麻疹。使用常规收集的通知、实验室、人口统计和 疫苗接种 信息建立了 麻疹 行列表。使用 血清学 和 PCR 定义实验室确诊病例。发病率 率是使用 2024 年中期人口估计值计算的。严重程度是通过与急诊科出勤率、入院率和住院时间的联系来评估的。结果 2,821 例实验室确诊病例中的大多数居住在伦敦(n = 1,283;45.5%)和西米德兰兹郡(n = 710;25.2%)。发病率 在伦敦最高(14.1/100,000 人口)。10 岁以下儿童占病例的 58.6% (n = 1,653)。大多数病例(n = 2,609;92.5%)没有接种疫苗,63.0%(n = 1,776)居住在属于最贫困的两个五分之一的地区。在可与住院数据相关的 2,788 例病例中,76.3% (n = 2,127) 接受了急诊护理,38.2% (n = 1,066) 入院。中位住院时间为2.0天,7例接受重症监护;据报告有 1 人死亡。结论 该 暴发 反映了英格兰高度贫困和 疫苗 吸收率低的地区向地方性 麻疹 传播 的转变。它强调了在所有地区和社区实现 95% 的 麻疹 疫苗 吸收率对于实现和维持消除的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
2023年10月至2024年9月期间,英格兰经历了数十年来最大规模的麻疹 暴发。本研究旨在描述 流行病学 的 流行病学、二级护理服务的负担以及导致这种死灰复燃的因素。
设计与对象
英国卫生安全局 (UKHSA) 对英格兰所有疑似 麻疹 病例进行了强化 麻疹 处理。该研究包括 2,821 例实验室确诊病例,其中大多数病例发生在伦敦和西米德兰兹郡。10岁以下儿童占病例的58.6%。
核心方法
使用常规收集的通知、实验室、人口统计和 疫苗接种 信息建立了 麻疹 行列表。使用 血清学 和 PCR 定义实验室确诊病例。严重程度是通过与急诊科出勤率、入院率和住院时间的联系来评估的。
主要结果
2,821 例实验室确诊病例中的大多数居住在伦敦(n = 1,283;45.5%)和西米德兰兹郡(n = 710;25.2%)。发病率 在伦敦最高(14.1/100,000 人口)。在可与住院数据相关的 2,788 例病例中,76.3% (n = 2,127) 接受了急诊护理,38.2% (n = 1,066) 入院。中位住院时间为2.0天,7例接受重症监护;据报告有 1 人死亡。
解释与创新
该 暴发 反映了英格兰高度贫困和 疫苗 吸收率低的地区向地方性 麻疹 传播 的转变。它强调了在所有地区和社区实现 95% 的 麻疹 疫苗 吸收率对于实现和维持消除的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,需要在高度贫困地区开展有针对性的 麻疹 活动,以防止未来出现 麻疹 并维持 麻疹 的消除。他们还为 监测 的公共卫生策略以及 疫苗 覆盖率较低的社区的应对措施提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于常规收集的数据,不包括详细的统计分析或因果解释。由于依赖 监测 数据且存在漏报的可能性,证据强度中等。

Measles resurgence in England: challenges in maintaining elimination, 2023 to 2024

Authors: Erjola Hani, Tishya Venkatraman, Julia Stowe, Rebecca Cordery, Mary Ramsay, Vanessa Saliba
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Between October 2023 and September 2024, England experienced the largest measles outbreak in decades. AIM We describe the epidemiology of the outbreak, burden on secondary care services and the factors that led to this resurgence. METHODS The UK Health Security Agency (UKHSA) conducts enhanced surveillance of all suspected measles cases in England. A measles line list was established using routinely collected notification, laboratory, demographic and vaccination information. Laboratory-confirmed cases were defined using serology and PCR. Incidence rates were calculated using mid-2024 population estimates. Severity was assessed through linkage with emergency department attendances, hospital admissions and length of stay. RESULTS Most of the 2,821 laboratory-confirmed cases resided in London (n = 1,283; 45.5%) and in the West Midlands (n = 710; 25.2%). Incidence was highest in London (14.1/100,000 population). Children under 10 years represented 58.6% (n = 1,653) of cases. Most cases (n = 2,609; 92.5%) were not vaccinated, and 63.0% (n = 1,776) lived in areas belonging to the two most deprived quintiles. Of the 2,788 cases that could be linked to hospitalisation data, 76.3% (n = 2,127) attended emergency care and 38.2% (n = 1,066) were admitted. Median length of stay was 2.0 days, seven cases received intensive care; one death was reported. Admissions were highest among infants aged CONCLUSION This outbreak reflects a shift towards endemic measles transmission in areas of high deprivation and low vaccine uptake in England. It underscores the importance of achieving 95% uptake with measles vaccine in all areas and communities to achieve and maintain elimination.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Between October 2023 and September 2024, England experienced the largest measles outbreak in decades. This study aimed to describe the epidemiology of the outbreak, burden on secondary care services and the factors that led to this resurgence.
Design and population
The UK Health Security Agency (UKHSA) conducted enhanced surveillance of all suspected measles cases in England. The study included 2,821 laboratory-confirmed cases, with most cases occurring in London and the West Midlands. Children under 10 years represented 58.6% of cases.
Core methods
A measles line list was established using routinely collected notification, laboratory, demographic and vaccination information. Laboratory-confirmed cases were defined using serology and PCR. Severity was assessed through linkage with emergency department attendances, hospital admissions and length of stay.
Main results
Most of the 2,821 laboratory-confirmed cases resided in London (n = 1,283; 45.5%) and in the West Midlands (n = 710; 25.2%). Incidence was highest in London (14.1/100,000 population). Of the 2,788 cases that could be linked to hospitalisation data, 76.3% (n = 2,127) attended emergency care and 38.2% (n = 1,066) were admitted. Median length of stay was 2.0 days, seven cases received intensive care; one death was reported.
Interpretation and novelty
This outbreak reflects a shift towards endemic measles transmission in areas of high deprivation and low vaccine uptake in England. It underscores the importance of achieving 95% uptake with measles vaccine in all areas and communities to achieve and maintain elimination.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for targeted vaccination campaigns in high-deprivation areas to prevent future outbreaks and maintain measles elimination. They also inform public health strategies for surveillance and response in communities with low vaccine coverage.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on routinely collected data and does not include detailed statistical analyses or causal interpretations. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the reliance on surveillance data and the potential for underreporting.

麻疹 2026 年在孟加拉国卷土重来破坏了消除成果,需要采取紧急政策行动

Measles resurgence in Bangladesh 2026 undermines elimination gains and requires urgent policy action
作者： Md. Abdus Salam
摘要中文翻译这些差异可能反映了数据来源和方法的差异。文章摘要 2026 年 麻疹 在孟加拉国卷土重来,凸显了免疫系统的脆弱性,揭示了由于 麻疹 覆盖率下降和系统性故障,消除疾病的成果可能会很快被逆转。本文概述了 暴发 的驱动因素和后果,强调迫切需要进行政策改革,重点关注公平的 疫苗 获取、改善治理和强有力的免疫策略,以预防未来危机。摘要 孟加拉国正在经历几十年来最严重的 麻疹 麻疹,在 2026 年 3 月中旬至 5 月中旬的报告期内,有超过 50,500 例疑似病例,至少 415 例确诊或疑似 麻疹 相关死亡。经过多年的进展,麻疹 麻疹 急剧减少,这种复苏并不反映生物学上的必然性但系统故障是可以预防的。2024 年至 2025 年全国范围内的 麻疹-风疹 (MR) 疫苗 缺货、自 2020 年以来缺乏全国性补充免疫活动 (SIA)、MR1 和 MR2 覆盖率下降以及国家以下地区不平等现象不断扩大,这些因素结合在一起,削弱了人口免疫力。这一负担不成比例地落在城市贫民窟、难以到达的农村地区和难民营的零剂量和疫苗接种不足的儿童身上,特别是对于常规 疫苗接种 而言太小的婴儿。在新冠疫情后更广泛的全球复苏中,孟加拉国的疫情凸显了 麻疹 是免疫弹性减弱的前哨。这场危机凸显了面临覆盖率下降的国家的三个可借鉴的经验教训:维持缓冲库存和多元化采购以防止缺货,在常规覆盖率下降时优先考虑追赶性的 SIA,以及嵌入以股权为重点的...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究涉及孟加拉国 麻疹 的死灰复燃,这破坏了之前的消除成果,需要采取紧急政策行动。尽管存在 疫苗,但背景强调了 麻疹 在全球范围内的持续威胁,以及实现高 疫苗接种 覆盖率以中断 传播 的重要性。
设计与对象
该研究考察了孟加拉国 麻疹 消除成果的逆转,重点关注 2000 年代初至 2026 年期间。它包括孟加拉国的儿童人口,通过对扩大免疫计划 (EPI) 的持续投资、与儿童健康日的联系以及重复的全国补充免疫活动 (SIA) 收集数据。
核心方法
这些方法涉及对国家和世卫组织 监测 数据的分析,包括包含 麻疹 的 疫苗(MCV1 和 MCV2)的覆盖率估计,以及对报告病例和死亡的检查。该研究还考虑了采购安排的变化和行政延误对 疫苗 缺货和常规免疫接种的影响。
主要结果
主要结果显示,2026年初麻疹病例和死亡人数显着增加,疑似病例50,500例,确诊或疑似麻疹相关死亡415例。大多数病例发生在五岁以下儿童,其中最集中在达卡区。该研究还报告了常规免疫覆盖率的下降和 SIA 的缺乏,这导致了 麻疹 的死灰复燃。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为孟加拉国 麻疹 消除工作的一个重大漏洞,覆盖范围不理想且错过了 SIA,从而造成了易感人群。该研究强调需要采取紧急政策行动来解决这些问题并防止进一步的 暴发。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果警告说,麻疹 在孟加拉国的死灰复燃对公共卫生具有重大影响,包括进一步传播的可能性以及加强 监测 和 疫苗接种 工作的必要性。该研究强调了保持高 疫苗接种 覆盖率以预防 麻疹 暴发 的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究承认其局限性,包括覆盖范围估计不一致以及延迟识别 麻疹 发病率 早期增加的可能性。由于依赖报告的数据且缺乏具体的方法学细节,证据强度中等。

Measles resurgence in Bangladesh 2026 undermines elimination gains and requires urgent policy action

Authors: Md. Abdus Salam
Original abstract (English)
These discrepancies may reflect differences in data sources and methods. Article summary The resurgence of measles in Bangladesh in 2026 highlights the fragility of immunization systems, revealing how quickly gains in disease elimination can be reversed due to declining vaccination coverage and systemic failures. This paper outlines the outbreak's drivers and consequences, emphasizing the urgent need for policy reforms focused on equitable vaccine access, improved governance, and robust immunization strategies to prevent future crises. Abstract Bangladesh is experiencing its worst measles outbreak in decades, with more than 50,500 suspected cases and at least 415 confirmed or suspected measles-related deaths during the reporting period from mid-March to mid-May 2026. After years of progress that had sharply reduced measles incidence, this resurgence reflects not a biological inevitability but a preventable systems failure. A nationwide Measles-Rubella (MR) vaccine stockout in 2024 to 2025, the absence of nationwide supplementary immunization activities (SIAs) since 2020, declining MR1 and MR2 coverage, and widening subnational inequities have combined to erode population immunity. The burden has fallen disproportionately on zero-dose and under-vaccinated children in urban slums, hard-to-reach rural areas, and refugee camps, especially infants too young for routine vaccination. Positioned within the broader post-COVID global resurgence, Bangladesh’s epidemic highlights measles as a sentinel of weakening immunization resilience. The crisis underscores three transferable lessons for countries facing declining coverage: maintain buffer stocks and diversified procurement to prevent stockouts, prioritize catch-up SIAs when routine coverage falters, and embed equity-focused o...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the resurgence of measles in Bangladesh, which undermines previous elimination gains and necessitates urgent policy action. The background highlights the ongoing threat of measles globally, despite the availability of a vaccine, and the importance of achieving high vaccination coverage to interrupt transmission.
Design and population
The study examines the reversal of Bangladesh’s measles elimination gains, focusing on the period from the early 2000s to 2026. It includes a population of children in Bangladesh, with data collected through sustained investment in the Expanded Programme on Immunization (EPI), linkage with child health days, and repeated nationwide supplementary immunization activities (SIAs).
Core methods
The methods involve the analysis of national and WHO surveillance data, including coverage estimates of measles-containing vaccines (MCV1 and MCV2), and the examination of reported cases and deaths. The study also considers the impact of changes in procurement arrangements and administrative delays on vaccine stockouts and routine immunization.
Main results
The main results show a significant increase in measles cases and deaths in early 2026, with 50,500 suspected cases and 415 confirmed or suspected measles-related deaths. The majority of cases occurred in children under five years of age, with a high concentration in Dhaka division. The study also reports a decline in routine immunization coverage and the absence of SIAs, which contributed to the resurgence of measles.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as indicating a major vulnerability in Bangladesh’s measles elimination efforts, with suboptimal coverage and missed SIAs creating a susceptible population. The study highlights the need for urgent policy action to address these issues and prevent further outbreaks.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings caution that the resurgence of measles in Bangladesh has significant implications for public health, including the potential for further spread and the need for enhanced surveillance and vaccination efforts. The study underscores the importance of maintaining high vaccination coverage to prevent measles outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study acknowledges limitations, including inconsistent coverage estimates and the potential for delays in recognizing early increases in measles incidence. The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on reported data and the absence of specific methodological details.

麻疹 诱发的 亚急性硬化性全脑炎(道森病):临床特征和诊断

MEASLES-INDUCED SUBACUTE SCLEROSING PANENCEPHALITIS (DAWSON'S DISEASE): CLINICAL CHARACTERISTICS AND DIAGNOSTICS
作者： Marine Goginashvili
摘要中文翻译简介:麻疹的特点是传染性高。亚急性硬化性全脑炎 (SSPE) 是其最罕见但最致命的并发症之一,主要发生在儿童和青少年中。感染者中的发生频率为 1:10,000,而在接种疫苗的个体中,这种情况极为罕见(~1:500,000)。麻疹 和病程:该疾病的主要特征是从感染 麻疹 到全脑炎发作之间有一段异常漫长的 6-15 年无症状期。病毒在神经组织中的持续存在和 免疫应答 的破坏导致精神神经损伤逐渐恶化。临床过程包括4个阶段:行为改变和头痛、癫痫发作、严重运动协调障碍(共济失调、肌阵挛)以及脑干损伤引起的昏迷,导致致命结果(平均3年内)。诊断:麻疹 基于病史、脑电图 (EEG) 上的特征性 Radermecker 复合物、脑脊液和血液中 麻疹 特异性 IgG 抗体滴度急剧升高以及 MRI 显示的脱髓鞘情况。结论:全脑炎是麻疹最严重的并发症。尽管有现代诊断方法,但这种疾病是进行性和致命性的,通用 疫苗接种 仍然是唯一有效的预防措施。

MEASLES-INDUCED SUBACUTE SCLEROSING PANENCEPHALITIS (DAWSON'S DISEASE): CLINICAL CHARACTERISTICS AND DIAGNOSTICS

Authors: Marine Goginashvili
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Measles is characterized by high contagiousness. One of its rarest but most lethal complications is subacute sclerosing panencephalitis (SSPE), which primarily occurs in children and adolescents. The frequency among infected individuals is 1:10,000, while in vaccinated individuals, it is exceptionally rare (~1:500,000). Pathogenesis and Course: The main feature of the disease is an atypically long, 6–15-year asymptomatic period between the contracted measles infection and the onset of panencephalitis. The persistence of the virus in neural tissues and the disruption of the immune response lead to progressively worsening psychoneurological damage. The clinical course includes 4 stages: behavioral changes and headaches, seizures, severe movement coordination disorders (ataxia, myoclonus), and coma caused by brainstem damage, leading to a fatal outcome (on average within 3 years). Diagnostics: The diagnosis is based on the medical history, characteristic Radermecker complexes on the electroencephalogram (EEG), a sharply elevated titer of measles-specific IgG antibodies in the cerebrospinal fluid and blood, and demyelination revealed by MRI. Conclusion: Panencephalitis represents the most severe complication of measles. Despite modern diagnostic methods, the disease is progressive and fatal, with universal vaccination remaining its only effective prevention measure.

Measles Vaccination Coverage and Vaccine Exemptions Before Ontario’s 2024–2025 Outbreak: An Analysis of Case Rates Across Public Health Units

Authors: Daniel Fosu Twum
Original abstract (English)
Time-stamped analysis plan for an exploratory ecological study of measles vaccination coverage, vaccine exemptions and outbreak case rates across Ontario’s 29 public health units during the 2024–2025 measles outbreak. The study uses publicly available aggregate data from Public Health Ontario (outbreak cases by public health unit; school-based measles coverage and exemption estimates for 7- and 17-year-olds) and Statistics Canada (population estimates). The plan was lodged before any statistical modelling. Public health unit exposure values and outbreak case rates had already been inspected, which prompted the documented amendment in version 2 (removal of the primary/secondary exposure hierarchy and addition of data-quality caveats). This is therefore not a fully prospective preregistration, and all association analyses are exploratory.

运动障碍为主的 亚急性硬化性全脑炎:视频案例

Movement Disorder-dominant Subacute Sclerosing Panencephalitis: A Video Case
作者： Sedat Işıkay
摘要中文翻译最近,Garg 等人的一项系统综述强调了 SSPE 中运动障碍现象学的显着异质性,并强调了识别非典型表现的重要性。然而,基底神经节、丘脑和皮质纹状体网络的参与被认为发挥着核心作用。[]神经病理学研究已经证明基底神经节回路内的变性和炎症变化,这可能导致多动性运动障碍的发展。[]神经影像学研究进一步表明,丘脑和基底神经节功能障碍可能是肌张力障碍、舞蹈症和其他异常运动出现的基础。神经影像学结果可能完全正常,特别是在疾病的早期阶段。[]我们的患者没有 MRI 异常,这可能反映了仍低于传统成像技术检测阈值的微观病理变化。[]本病例特别值得注意的一个方面是运动障碍表现的显着优势。总之,该视频病例说明 SSPE 不仅仅是一种以周期性肌阵挛为特征的脑炎性疾病,而且还可能表现为以运动障碍为主的表型。尊敬的编辑,亚急性硬化性全脑炎 (SSPE) 是一种进行性、致命性的神经退行性疾病,由突变的 麻疹病毒 持续感染中枢神经系统引起。[]该疾病通常表现为认知能力下降、行为恶化、学习成绩进行性损害和刻板的周期性肌阵挛。[,]然而,越来越多的证据表明 SSPE 的临床谱比以前认识的要广泛得多...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在强调 亚急性硬化性全脑炎 (SSPE) 更广泛的临床谱,特别关注运动障碍主导临床表现的病例,这对诊断准确性提出了挑战。作者试图强调识别 SSPE 非典型表现的重要性,特别是在正常脑部 MRI 结果的背景下以及脑电图 (EEG) 在 诊断 中的关键作用。
设计与对象
该研究涉及一名 9 岁男孩 病例报告,该男孩出现进行性步态不稳定和不自主运动,最初被认为是原发性运动障碍、神经退行性疾病或自身免疫性脑炎。患者接受了临床评估、神经系统检查和脑电图 (EEG),MRI 显示正常结果。SSPE 的 麻疹 通过脑脊液和血清中抗 麻疹 抗体滴度升高得到证实。
核心方法
通过临床评估、神经学检查和脑电图对患者进行评估。进行 MRI 检查以排除结构异常。
主要结果
患者表现出进行性认知恶化、行为改变和频繁的不自主运动,包括肌阵挛性抽搐和肌张力障碍姿势。EEG 显示特征性周期性放电,抗 麻疹 抗体滴度升高证实了 SSPE 麻疹。
解释与创新
该研究强调了识别 SSPE 的非典型表现的重要性,特别是当运动障碍主导临床表现时。作者强调了脑电图在诊断 SSPE 中的关键作用,即使没有典型的 MRI 结果。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果警告不要根据正常 MRI 结果排除 SSPE,并强调在区分进行性运动障碍和认知衰退的 诊断 时考虑 SSPE 的必要性。该研究还强调了脑电图在诊断 SSPE 中的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到其单一 病例报告 设计的限制,无法推广。然而,特征性脑电图结果和升高的 麻疹 特异性抗体滴度支持了这些发现,为 SSPE 的 麻疹 提供了强有力的证据。

Movement Disorder-dominant Subacute Sclerosing Panencephalitis: A Video Case

Authors: Sedat Işıkay
Original abstract (English)
More recently, a systematic review by Garg et al.[] highlighted the remarkable heterogeneity of movement disorder phenomenology in SSPE and underscored the importance of recognizing atypical presentations. However, involvement of the basal ganglia, thalamus, and cortico-striatal networks is believed to play a central role.[] Neuropathological studies have demonstrated degeneration and inflammatory changes within basal ganglia circuits, which may contribute to the development of hyperkinetic movement disorders.[] Neuroimaging studies have further suggested that thalamic and basal ganglia dysfunction may underlie the emergence of dystonia, chorea, and other abnormal movements.[,] Nevertheless, structural neuroimaging findings may be entirely normal, particularly during the early stages of the disease.[] The absence of MRI abnormalities in our patient likely reflects microscopic pathological changes that remain below the detection threshold of conventional imaging techniques.[] A particularly noteworthy aspect of the present case was the striking predominance of movement disorder manifestations. In conclusion, this video case illustrates that SSPE is not merely an encephalitic disorder characterized by periodic myoclonus but may also present with a movement disorder-dominant phenotype. Dear Editor, Subacute sclerosing panencephalitis (SSPE) is a progressive and fatal neurodegenerative disorder caused by persistent infection of the central nervous system by a mutated measles virus.[] The disease classically presents with cognitive decline, behavioral deterioration, progressive impairment of scholastic performance, and stereotyped periodic myoclonus.[,] However, accumulating evidence indicates that the clinical spectrum of SSPE is considerably broader than previously recogn...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to highlight the broader clinical spectrum of subacute sclerosing panencephalitis (SSPE), particularly focusing on cases where movement disorders dominate the clinical presentation, challenging diagnostic accuracy. The authors sought to emphasize the importance of recognizing atypical presentations of SSPE, especially in the context of normal brain MRI findings and the critical role of electroencephalography (EEG) in diagnosis.
Design and population
The study involved a single case report of a 9-year-old boy presenting with progressive gait instability and involuntary movements, initially considered for primary movement disorders, neurodegenerative diseases, or autoimmune encephalitis. The patient underwent clinical evaluation, neurological examination, and electroencephalography (EEG), with MRI showing normal findings. The diagnosis of SSPE was confirmed by elevated anti-measles antibody titers in cerebrospinal fluid and serum.
Core methods
The patient was evaluated through clinical assessment, neurological examination, and EEG. MRI was performed to rule out structural abnormalities.
Main results
The patient exhibited progressive cognitive deterioration, behavioral changes, and frequent involuntary movements, including myoclonic jerks and dystonic posturing. EEG revealed characteristic periodic discharges, and elevated anti-measles antibody titers confirmed the SSPE diagnosis.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlighted the importance of recognizing atypical presentations of SSPE, particularly when movement disorders dominate the clinical picture. The authors emphasized the critical role of EEG in diagnosing SSPE, even in the absence of typical MRI findings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings caution against excluding SSPE based on normal MRI results and underscore the necessity of considering SSPE in the differential diagnosis of progressive movement disorders and cognitive decline. The study also highlights the importance of EEG in diagnosing SSPE.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its single case report design, which does not allow for generalization. However, the findings are supported by characteristic EEG findings and elevated measles-specific antibody titers, providing strong evidence for the diagnosis of SSPE.
