使用 Holt-Winters、SARIMA 和混合 SARIMA-ANN 模型对尼日利亚河流州五岁以下儿童的 麻疹 发病率 进行比较建模

Comparative Modeling of Measles Incidences Among Children Below Five Years in Rivers State, Nigeria Using the Holt-Winters, SARIMA, and Hybrid SARIMA-ANN Models
作者： Amala Isabella Smart
摘要中文翻译本研究利用来自河流州医院管理委员会和联盟合作伙伴的 2015 年 1 月至 2024 年 12 月期间的月度数据,分析了尼日利亚河流州 5 岁以下儿童中 麻疹 发生的各种时间序列预测模型。数据分析使用了 Python 3.11 软件。研究重点是 麻疹 病例的时间模式,并评估了加法 Holt-Winters、乘法 Holt-Winters、季节性自回归综合移动平均 (SARIMA) 和混合 SARIMA-人工神经网络 (ANN) 模型的预测效果。为了探索数据系列,使用了描述性统计、季节性分解、Jarque-Bera 正态性​​检验、增强的 Dickey-Fuller 单位根评估、自相关评估和 Ljung-Box 检验等方法。预测效率通过均方根误差(RMSE)、平均绝对比例误差(MASE)和平均绝对百分比误差(MAPE)来衡量。调查结果显示,平均每月有 26.17 起 麻疹 病例,标准差为 21.43,峰值为 124 例。数据系列显示出正偏度,不符合正态分布,而增强迪基-富勒检验的结果表明在显着性水平上具有平稳性(p < 0.001)。Ljung-Box 分析发现滞后 12 和 20 处存在显着的序列相关性 (p < 0.001)。在四种预测技术中,乘法 Holt-Winters 方法产生了最好的结果,达到了 16.43 的最低 RMSE,报告的 MASE 为 86.68。相反,混合 SARIMA-ANN 方法效率最低,RMSE 为 22.05,报告的 MASE 为 261.66。研究得出的结论是,乘法霍尔特-温特斯方法是测试模型中最有效的模型。它建议验证报告的 MASE 值、一致的样本外评估的重要性,以及加强定期 麻疹 监测以帮助及时预测、疫苗接种 策略和有针对性的公共卫生行动。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究利用来自河流州医院管理委员会和联盟合作伙伴的 2015 年 1 月至 2024 年 12 月期间的月度数据,分析了尼日利亚河流州 5 岁以下儿童中 麻疹 发生的各种时间序列预测模型。该研究旨在评估加法 Holt-Winters、乘法 Holt-Winters、季节性自回归综合移动平均 (SARIMA) 和混合的预测效果。SARIMA——人工神经网络 (ANN) 模型。
设计与对象
调查重点关注麻疹病例的时间模式,并使用2015年1月至2024年12月的月度数据评估四种模型的预测效果。使用Python 3.11软件进行数据分析。
核心方法
使用描述性统计、季节分解、Jarque-Bera 正态性​​检验、增强的 Dickey-Fuller 单位根评估、自相关评估和 Ljung-Box 检验对数据系列进行了探索。预测效率通过均方根误差 (RMSE)、平均绝对比例误差 (MASE) 和平均绝对百分比误差 (MAPE) 来衡量。
主要结果
数据系列呈现正偏度,不符合正态分布。增强迪基-富勒检验表明显着性水平上的平稳性 (p < 0.001)。Ljung-Box 分析发现滞后 12 和 20 处存在显着的序列相关性 (p < 0.001)。乘法 Holt-Winters 方法产生了最好的结果,达到了最低的 RMSE,为 16.43,报告的 MASE 为 86.68,而混合 SARIMA-ANN 方法效果最差,RMSE 为 22.05,报告的 MASE 为 261.66。
解释与创新
研究得出的结论是,乘法霍尔特-温特斯方法是测试模型中最有效的模型。它建议验证报告的 MASE 值、一致的样本外评估的重要性,以及加强定期 麻疹 监测以帮助及时预测、疫苗接种 策略和有针对性的公共卫生行动。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为开发更准确的 麻疹 预测模型提供信息,从而有助于及时制定 麻疹 策略和公共卫生干预措施。该研究强调需要对 麻疹 病例进行一致的样本外评估和定期监测。
局限与证据强度
该研究的结果基于特定的数据集,可能无法推广到其他地区或人群。

Comparative Modeling of Measles Incidences Among Children Below Five Years in Rivers State, Nigeria Using the Holt-Winters, SARIMA, and Hybrid SARIMA-ANN Models

Authors: Amala Isabella Smart
Original abstract (English)
This research analyzed various time-series forecasting models for measles occurrences in children younger than five in Rivers State, Nigeria, utilizing monthly data sourced from the Rivers State Hospital Management Board and allied partners spanning from January 2015 to December 2024. Python 3.11 software was used for the data analysis. The investigation focused on the temporal patterns of measles cases and assessed the prediction efficacy of additive Holt–Winters, multiplicative Holt–Winters, Seasonal Autoregressive Integrated Moving Average (SARIMA), and hybrid SARIMA–Artificial Neural Network (ANN) models. To explore the data series, methods such as descriptive statistics, seasonal decomposition, the Jarque–Bera normality examination, the Augmented Dickey–Fuller unit-root evaluation, autocorrelation assessment, and the Ljung–Box test were utilized. The forecasting efficiency was measured via root mean squared error (RMSE), mean absolute scaled error (MASE), and mean absolute percentage error (MAPE). Findings indicated an average of 26.17 monthly cases of measles, with a standard deviation of 21.43 and a peak of 124 cases. The data series displayed positive skewness and did not conform to a normal distribution, while the results from the Augmented Dickey–Fuller test suggested stationarity at level significance (p < 0.001). The Ljung–Box analysis uncovered significant serial correlations at lags 12 and 20 (p < 0.001). Out of the four forecasting techniques, the multiplicative Holt–Winters method yielded the best results, achieving the lowest RMSE at 16.43 and a reported MASE of 86.68. Conversely, the hybrid SARIMA–ANN approach was the least effective, presenting an RMSE of 22.05 and a reported MASE of 261.66. The study concludes that the multiplicative Holt–Winters method stands out as the most effective model among those tested. It suggests the validation of the reported MASE values, the importance of consistent out-of-sample evaluation, and enhancing regular measles monitoring to aid in timely forecasting, vaccination strategy, and focused public health actions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This research analyzed various time-series forecasting models for measles occurrences in children younger than five in Rivers State, Nigeria, utilizing monthly data sourced from the Rivers State Hospital Management Board and allied partners spanning from January 2015 to December 2024. The study aimed to assess the prediction efficacy of additive Holt–Winters, multiplicative Holt–Winters, Seasonal Autoregressive Integrated Moving Average (SARIMA), and hybrid SARIMA–Artificial Neural Network (ANN) models.
Design and population
The investigation focused on the temporal patterns of measles cases and assessed the prediction efficacy of four models using monthly data from January 2015 to December 2024. Python 3.11 software was used for the data analysis.
Core methods
The data series was explored using descriptive statistics, seasonal decomposition, the Jarque–Bera normality examination, the Augmented Dickey–Fuller unit-root evaluation, autocorrelation assessment, and the Ljung–Box test. Forecasting efficiency was measured via root mean squared error (RMSE), mean absolute scaled error (MASE), and mean absolute percentage error (MAPE).
Main results
The data series displayed positive skewness and did not conform to a normal distribution. The Augmented Dickey–Fuller test suggested stationarity at level significance (p < 0.001). The Ljung–Box analysis uncovered significant serial correlations at lags 12 and 20 (p < 0.001). The multiplicative Holt–Winters method yielded the best results, achieving the lowest RMSE at 16.43 and a reported MASE of 86.68, while the hybrid SARIMA–ANN approach was the least effective, presenting an RMSE of 22.05 and a reported MASE of 261.66.
Interpretation and novelty
The study concludes that the multiplicative Holt–Winters method stands out as the most effective model among those tested. It suggests the validation of the reported MASE values, the importance of consistent out-of-sample evaluation, and enhancing regular measles monitoring to aid in timely forecasting, vaccination strategy, and focused public health actions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more accurate forecasting models for measles, which can aid in timely vaccination strategies and public health interventions. The study highlights the need for consistent out-of-sample evaluation and regular monitoring of measles cases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's findings are based on a specific dataset and may not be generalizable to other regions or populations.

首次服用含 麻疹 的 疫苗 时的年龄对 麻疹 体液免疫的影响:一项回顾性 队列研究 研究,加拿大,2014 年至 2016 年

The effect of age at first dose of a measles-containing vaccine on measles humoral immunity: a retrospective cohort study, Canada, 2014 to 2016
作者： Archchun Ariyarajah, Natasha S Crowcroft, Kevin A Brown, John Wang, Jeffrey C Kwong, Shelly Bolotin
摘要中文翻译背景 加拿大于 1998 年淘汰了 麻疹;然而,最近报道了大的 麻疹,并且在 2025 年失去了消除状态。目的 我们的目的是评估 麻疹 含有 麻疹 (MCV1) 的第一剂年龄对 麻疹 IgG 浓度和血清阴性的影响,在 麻疹 近二十年后的两剂接受者中。方法 我们对 2014 年至 2016 年在加拿大安大略省进行了 麻疹 IgG 检测的 10,766 名两次接种疫苗的个体进行了回顾性 队列研究 研究。我们使用结果阈值预测了 麻疹 IgG 浓度和血清阴性。MCV1 年龄增加 1 个月(12 至 15 个月之间)与消除后出生的女性的预测平均 麻疹 IgG 浓度增加 13.7%(95% 置信区间 (CI):7.1–20.8)相关,与 9.2%(95% CI: 6.8–11.6) 对于淘汰前出生的女性。根据血清阴性阈值和出生时 麻疹 消除状态,MCV1 年龄增加 1 个月(12 至 15 个月之间)与血清阴性比值比降低相关,范围为 0.74(95% CI:0.63-0.85)至 0.87(95% CI:0.57-1.27)。使用最低阈值(≤ 120 mIU/mL)和最长随访期(MCV1 后 24 年),我们预测 15 个月时接种 MCV1 的女性中有 4.1% (95% CI: 2.7–6.1) 血清阴性,而 12 个月时接种 MCV1 的女性中这一比例为 9.6% (95% CI: 7.4–12.3)。讨论 将 MCV1 推迟到较大年龄(13-15 个月)可能会导致血清阴性个体的百分比绝对下降 6%,从而可能增加维持群体免疫力的可能性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 麻疹 含有 麻疹 (MCV1) 的第一剂年龄对 麻疹 IgG 浓度和 麻疹 在 疫苗接种 后近二十年的两剂接受者中血清阴性的影响。加拿大于1998年淘汰了麻疹,但最近报道了大型麻疹,并于2025年失去了淘汰地位。
设计与对象
一项回顾性 队列研究 研究对 2014 年至 2016 年在加拿大安大略省进行的 10,766 名两次接种疫苗的个体进行了 麻疹 IgG 检测。该研究重点关注 12 至 15 个月大时接种 MCV1 疫苗的女性。
核心方法
该研究采用回顾性 队列研究 设计,根据首次接种 MCV1 时的年龄来预测 麻疹 IgG 浓度和血清阴性。它分析了 2014 年至 2016 年在加拿大安大略省接受 麻疹 IgG 检测的个人的数据。
主要结果
MCV1 年龄增加 1 个月(12 至 15 个月之间)与消除后出生的女性的 13.7%(95% 置信区间 (CI):7.1–20.8)较高的预测平均 麻疹 IgG 浓度相关,而对于消除前出生的女性则与 9.2%(95% CI:6.8–11.6)较高的预测平均 麻疹 IgG 浓度相关。根据血清阴性阈值和出生时 麻疹 消除状态,MCV1 年龄增加 1 个月与血清阴性比值比降低相关,范围为 0.74 (95% CI: 0.63–0.85) 至 0.87 (95% CI: 0.57–1.27)。
解释与创新
作者认为,将 MCV1 延迟到较大年龄(13-15 个月)可能会导致血清阴性个体的百分比绝对下降 6%,从而可能增加维持群体免疫的可能性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为 麻疹 疫苗接种 计划的策略提供信息,特别是关于首次剂量的最佳时机,以确保足够的体液免疫并降低长期血清阴性的风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于回顾性数据,可能会受到此类设计固有的偏差的影响。证据是温和的,因为它依赖于统计预测而不是直接的实验验证。

The effect of age at first dose of a measles-containing vaccine on measles humoral immunity: a retrospective cohort study, Canada, 2014 to 2016

Authors: Archchun Ariyarajah, Natasha S Crowcroft, Kevin A Brown, John Wang, Jeffrey C Kwong, Shelly Bolotin
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Canada eliminated measles in 1998; however, large outbreaks have been recently reported, and elimination status was lost in 2025. AIM We aimed to estimate the effect of age at first dose of measles-containing vaccine (MCV1) on measles IgG concentration and seronegativity among two-dose recipients nearly two decades after vaccination. METHODS We conducted a retrospective cohort study of 10,766 twice-vaccinated individuals tested for measles IgG from 2014 to 2016 in Ontario, Canada. We predicted measles IgG concentration and seronegativity using thresholds of RESULTS A 1-month increase in age at MCV1 (between 12 and 15 months) was associated with 13.7% (95% confidence interval (CI): 7.1–20.8) higher predicted mean measles IgG concentration for females born after elimination and 9.2% (95% CI: 6.8–11.6) for females born before elimination. Depending on the seronegativity threshold and the measles elimination status at birth, a 1-month increase in age at MCV1 (between 12 and 15 months) was associated with lower odds ratio of seronegativity ranging from 0.74 (95% CI: 0.63–0.85) to 0.87 (95% CI: 0.57–1.27). Using the lowest threshold (≤ 120 mIU/mL) and the longest follow-up period (24 years post-MCV1), we predicted that 4.1% (95% CI: 2.7–6.1) of females vaccinated with MCV1 at 15 months of age would be seronegative, compared with 9.6% (95% CI: 7.4–12.3) of those vaccinated at 12 months. DISCUSSION Delaying MCV1 to older ages (13–15 months) may result in an absolute decrease as large as 6% in the percentage of seronegative individuals, potentially increasing the likelihood of maintaining herd immunity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to estimate the effect of age at first dose of measles-containing vaccine (MCV1) on measles IgG concentration and seronegativity among two-dose recipients nearly two decades after vaccination. Canada eliminated measles in 1998, but large outbreaks have been recently reported, and elimination status was lost in 2025.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study was conducted of 10,766 twice-vaccinated individuals tested for measles IgG from 2014 to 2016 in Ontario, Canada. The study focused on females vaccinated with MCV1 at 12 to 15 months of age.
Core methods
The study used a retrospective cohort design to predict measles IgG concentration and seronegativity based on age at first dose of MCV1. It analyzed data from individuals tested for measles IgG between 2014 and 2016 in Ontario, Canada.
Main results
A 1-month increase in age at MCV1 (between 12 and 15 months) was associated with 13.7% (95% confidence interval (CI): 7.1–20.8) higher predicted mean measles IgG concentration for females born after elimination and 9.2% (95% CI: 6.8–11.6) for females born before elimination. Depending on the seronegativity threshold and the measles elimination status at birth, a 1-month increase in age at MCV1 was associated with lower odds ratios of seronegativity ranging from 0.74 (95% CI: 0.63–0.85) to 0.87 (95% CI: 0.57–1.27).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors suggest that delaying MCV1 to older ages (13–15 months) may result in an absolute decrease as large as 6% in the percentage of seronegative individuals, potentially increasing the likelihood of maintaining herd immunity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform strategies for measles vaccination programs, particularly regarding optimal timing of the first dose to ensure adequate humoral immunity and reduce the risk of seronegativity in the long term.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on retrospective data and may be subject to biases inherent in such designs. The evidence is moderate as it relies on statistical predictions rather than direct experimental validation.

2025 年巴西流行病学疫苗接种对 麻疹 根除和 发病率 流行病学 麻疹 的影响2025 年巴西免疫接种影响和萨拉皮翁根除及布罗特斯流行病事件的影响 EN 2025

IMPACTO DA IMUNIZAÇÃO NA ERRADICAÇÃO DO SARAMPO E A INCIDÊNCIA DE SURTOS EPIDEMIOLÓGICOS NO BRASIL EM 2025 THE IMPACT OF IMMUNIZATION ON MEASLES ERADICATION AND THE INCIDENCE OF EPIDEMIOLOGICAL OUTBREAKS IN BRAZIL IN 2025 EL IMPACTO DE LA INMUNIZACIÓN EN LA ERRADICACIÓN DEL SARAMPIÓN Y LA INCIDENCIA DE BROTES EPIDEMIOLÓGICOS EN BRASIL EN 2025
作者： Felipe Vieira Moura, Vinicius Soares de Sousa, Christiane Rodrigues de Paula
摘要中文翻译巴西流行病学调查结果显示,2025 年的流行病学观察结果与疫苗接种有关。回顾和量化堕胎,从根本上了解预防和免疫方面的官方警戒系统,考虑 2020 年和 2025 年期间的登记。分布时间和 surtos 的 Ocorrência,com destaque para o estado do do tocantins。2021 年的情况将减少,2023 年的确认和 2024 年和 2025 年的新登记的后验。2025 年,将不再有坎普斯林多斯市政、没有托坎廷斯,国际病毒介绍协会和个人展示。疫苗改革构成了传播传播的主要策略。结论是提高疫苗接种水平和保持疫苗一致性的措施,是流行病学警戒协会持续开展的工作,是巴西预防新冠肺炎疫情的必要措施和支持。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本文试图分析免疫接种对 麻疹 控制和根除的影响,将 疫苗接种 覆盖率与巴西流行病学 发病率 的 发病率 联系起来,重点关注 2025 年情景。
设计与对象
这是一项流行病学描述性、回顾性和定量研究,基于官方卫生和免疫监测系统的二手数据,涵盖2020年至2025年期间。
核心方法
数据分析包括确诊病例、疫苗接种 覆盖范围、时间分布和 暴发 发生情况的信息,重点关注 Tocantins 的状态。
主要结果
数据显示,2021年后病例大幅减少,2023年无确诊病例,随后2024年和2025年新增登记。
解释与创新
2025 年,托坎廷斯州坎波斯林多斯的病例激增与国际旅行和易感人群的存在有关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果证实,疫苗接种 是中断赝病毒 传播 的主要策略。
局限与证据强度
结论强调需要保持高且均匀的 疫苗接种 覆盖率以及持续的流行病学 监测,以防止新的 暴发 并维持巴西的疾病消除。

IMPACTO DA IMUNIZAÇÃO NA ERRADICAÇÃO DO SARAMPO E A INCIDÊNCIA DE SURTOS EPIDEMIOLÓGICOS NO BRASIL EM 2025 THE IMPACT OF IMMUNIZATION ON MEASLES ERADICATION AND THE INCIDENCE OF EPIDEMIOLOGICAL OUTBREAKS IN BRAZIL IN 2025 EL IMPACTO DE LA INMUNIZACIÓN EN LA ERRADICACIÓN DEL SARAMPIÓN Y LA INCIDENCIA DE BROTES EPIDEMIOLÓGICOS EN BRASIL EN 2025

Authors: Felipe Vieira Moura, Vinicius Soares de Sousa, Christiane Rodrigues de Paula
Original abstract (English)
Este artigo buscou analisar o impacto da imunização no controle e na eliminação do sarampo, relacionando a cobertura vacinal à incidência de surtos epidemiológicos no Brasil, com ênfase no cenário observado em 2025. Trata-se de um estudo epidemiológico descritivo, retrospectivo e de abordagem quantitativa, fundamentado em dados secundários provenientes de sistemas oficiais de vigilância em saúde e imunização, considerando registros do período de 2020 a 2025. Foram analisadas informações referentes aos casos confirmados, cobertura vacinal, distribuição temporal e ocorrência de surtos, com destaque para o estado do Tocantins. Os dados demonstraram redução expressiva dos casos após 2021, ausência de confirmações em 2023 e posterior ocorrência de novos registros em 2024 e 2025. Em 2025, destacou-se o surto ocorrido no município de Campos Lindos, no Tocantins, associado à introdução do vírus após viagem internacional e à presença de indivíduos suscetíveis. Os achados reforçam que a vacinação constitui a principal estratégia para interromper a transmissão do sarampo. Conclui-se que a manutenção de elevada e homogênea cobertura vacinal, associada à vigilância epidemiológica contínua, é indispensável para prevenir novos surtos e sustentar a eliminação da doença no Brasil.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This article sought to analyze the impact of immunization on measles control and eradication, linking vaccination coverage to the incidence of epidemiological outbreaks in Brazil, with a focus on the 2025 scenario.
Design and population
It was an epidemiological descriptive, retrospective, and quantitative study based on secondary data from official health and immunization surveillance systems, covering the period from 2020 to 2025.
Core methods
Data analysis included information on confirmed cases, vaccination coverage, temporal distribution, and outbreak occurrence, with emphasis on the state of Tocantins.
Main results
Data showed a significant reduction in cases after 2021, no confirmations in 2023, and subsequent new registrations in 2024 and 2025.
Interpretation and novelty
In 2025, a surge in Campos Lindos, Tocantins, was highlighted, associated with international travel and the presence of susceptible individuals.
Scientific and public-health significance
Findings reinforce that vaccination is the main strategy to interrupt sarsenavirus transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
Conclusions emphasize the need for maintaining high and homogeneous vaccination coverage, along with continuous epidemiological surveillance, to prevent new outbreaks and sustain disease elimination in Brazil.

查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 麻疹 消除的丧失以及该疾病在实施控制措施一个世纪后的死灰复燃。
设计与对象
该研究是一份预印本,提供了用于分析 麻疹 消除和控制后复苏的代码、记录和派生表格,摘要中没有提供具体的人群详细信息。

Project 004: code and reproduction package for loss of measles elimination and a century of post-control resurgence

Authors: Boyuan Wang, Shangkun Zhong, Le Yu, Xueqian Yi
Original abstract (English)
Code, records and derived tables of the study "Loss of measles elimination and a century of post-control resurgence" (Boyuan Wang, Shangkun Zhong, Le Yu, Xueqian Yi; Zhuhai Fudan Innovation Research Institute). Preregistration 2: https://osf.io/a4y2x; rapid preregistration: https://osf.io/xtwjc. The package keeps the project's folder layout, so every path in the registrations and in the lock records can be checked as written. It holds the code, data manifests (no third-party raw data), lock records with RFC 3161 time stamps, confirmatory outputs, derived tables, smoke tests and a script that recomputes the main result; see README.md in the package. Licence ("Other"): the terms are those of the licence files in the package. Code: MIT (LICENSE). Documentation, records and data summaries: CC BY 4.0 (LICENSE-docs.md). Values derived from WHO data remain under CC BY-NC-SA 3.0 IGO, and values derived from CShapes 2.0, the Global Transnational Mobility dataset and OpenFlights keep the terms of their sources (LICENSE-docs.md, DERIVED_TABLES.tsv). How to cite: CITATION.cff.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the loss of measles elimination and the resurgence of the disease a century after control measures were implemented.
Design and population
The study is a preprint that provides code, records, and derived tables for the analysis of measles elimination and post-control resurgence, with no specific population details provided in the abstract.
Core methods
The methods involve the use of a code package that includes data manifests, lock records with RFC 3161 timestamps, confirmatory outputs, derived tables, smoke tests, and a script to recompute the main result, as detailed in the README.md file.
Main results
The package contains the code, data summaries, and derived tables necessary to reproduce the main results of the study on measles elimination and post-control resurgence.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors state that the package provides a transparent and reproducible framework for analyzing the historical patterns of measles elimination and resurgence, which is novel in its approach to data and code sharing.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform public health strategies for measles control and surveillance by providing a reproducible method to analyze historical data and model disease resurgence.
Limitations and evidence strength

在控制后复苏的一个世纪中 麻疹 消除的损失:验证性分析的完全预注册(预注册 2 of 2)

Loss of measles elimination in a century of post-control resurgence: full preregistration of confirmatory analyses (preregistration 2 of 2)
作者： Shangkun Zhong, Xueqian Yi
摘要中文翻译这是一个项目两次预注册中的第二次(预注册 1:https://osf.io/xtwjc)。在运行任何验证性分析之前,它会注册假设及其支持和反驳标准、由 SHA-256 哈希值和带时间戳的锁定记录识别的冻结代码和配置文件、两个预先注册的模型搜索选择的配置、决策规则和证据门,以及 2023-2026 年测试期的密封。A 部分询问免疫性疾病控制后复发的幅度和发病风险是否遵循由易感出生人数积累驱动的普通规律(C1)。B 部分建立了 麻疹 消除状态丧失的提前时间分层风险评估 (H1),检查成人免疫差距 (H2) 和输入压力 (H3),并将 2025-26 麻疹 在美国的复苏置于控制后一个世纪内的复苏 (H4)。所有开发均使用截至 2022 年 12 月 31 日的数据。尚未计算测试期间的性能以进行任何分析,并且在该注册具有 OSF 注册号之前,代码拒绝计算它。文本中列出了注册前注册的偏差(DV01-DV19)、先前的数据访问以及注册后要发布的附录。

问题与背景

设计与对象
该研究是验证性分析的预注册,重点关注 麻疹病毒 的复苏模式以及 2025-2026 年美国的免疫差距和输入压力等相关因素。

科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为未来的 麻疹 和 麻疹 复苏的风险评估策略提供信息,特别是在免疫差距和输入压力的背景下。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于没有实际结果的预注册,并且由于缺乏计算的测试期表现,可信度中等。

Loss of measles elimination in a century of post-control resurgence: full preregistration of confirmatory analyses (preregistration 2 of 2)

Authors: Shangkun Zhong, Xueqian Yi
Original abstract (English)
This is the second of two preregistrations of one project (preregistration 1: https://osf.io/xtwjc). It registers, before any confirmatory analysis is run, the hypotheses with their support and refutation criteria, the frozen code and configuration files identified by SHA-256 hashes and timestamped lock records, the configurations selected by two pre-registered model searches, the decision rules and the evidence gate, and the sealing of the test period 2023–2026. Part A asks whether the amplitude and the onset hazard of resurgence after control of immunisable diseases follow a common law driven by the accumulation of susceptible births (C1). Part B builds a lead-time-stratified risk assessment of loss of measles elimination status (H1), examines adult immunity gaps (H2) and importation pressure (H3), and places the 2025–26 measles resurgence in the United States within a century of post-control resurgences (H4). All development used data up to 31 December 2022. Test-period performance has not been computed for any analysis, and the code refuses to compute it until this registration has an OSF registration number. Deviations registered before registration (DV01–DV19), prior data access and the addenda to be posted after registration are listed in the text.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study investigates the resurgence of measles after its elimination and aims to understand the common law governing such events, particularly focusing on the role of susceptible births.
Design and population
The study is a preregistration of confirmatory analyses, focusing on measles virus resurgence patterns and related factors such as immunity gaps and importation pressure in the United States during 2025–2026.
Core methods
The methods involve pre-registering hypotheses, using frozen code and configuration files, and selecting configurations through two pre-registered model searches, all based on data up to 31 December 2022.
Main results
The study does not report any direct results as the test-period performance has not been computed for any analysis.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the preregistration as a framework for future analysis, emphasizing the need for rigorous methods to assess measles resurgence and its underlying factors.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform future surveillance and risk assessment strategies for measles resurgence, particularly in the context of immunity gaps and importation pressures.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to preregistration without actual results, and the confidence is moderate due to the absence of computed test-period performance.

通过基于模拟的学习将微生物学和公共卫生联系起来。

Bridging microbiology and public health through simulation-based learning.
作者： Kathryn C. Krupinsky, Denise E. Kirschner
摘要中文翻译在我们以全球健康为中心的同步在线高级微生物学课程中,我们发现学生很难将学习内容转化为现实世界的应用。例如,考虑一下最近的 麻疹 暴发 和 COVID-19 大流行等重大事件,这引发了许多关于公共卫生专业人员如何使用基本微生物信息的问题。为了解决这些问题,我们创建了一个基于模拟的课程,让学生在传染病 暴发 期间扮演行动角色。我们的独立课程贯穿历史 麻疹 暴发,介绍 麻疹 调查、社区沟通以及这些如何依赖于微生物知识。通过分组,学生有机会决定分类、公共信息和干预指标。我们为学生提供 暴发 调查参考工作表,并将分组讨论室与涵盖背景信息的教学小插曲交织在一起,同时结合响应科学家获得的数据揭示对 暴发 的实际响应。学生综合材料并实时应用,让他们能够锻炼批判性思维。在小型高年级虚拟教室中进行的试点测试表明,该课程在提高对学习目标的掌握以及增强微生物学和公共卫生与大众媒体的相关性方面具有强大的潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决学生将微生物知识转化为现实应用的挑战,特别是在公共卫生紧急情况下,例如最近的 麻疹 暴发 和 COVID-19 大流行。
设计与对象
为高级微生物学课程开发了模拟课程,以历史麻疹 暴发为中心,介绍暴发的调查、社区交流和微生物知识的应用。
核心方法
该课程包括一个独立的模拟,演练历史 麻疹 暴发,使用分组进行决策和 暴发 调查参考工作表。
主要结果
在小型高年级虚拟教室中进行的试点测试表明,该课程在提高对学习目标的掌握以及增强微生物学和公共卫生与大众媒体的相关性方面具有强大的潜力。
解释与创新
学生能够综合材料并实时应用,锻炼批判性思维和决策技能。
科研与公卫意义
该课程有可能改善微生物学知识在公共卫生实践中的应用,特别是在管理传染病 暴发 方面。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于小型高年级虚拟教室,证据强度中等。

Bridging microbiology and public health through simulation-based learning.

Authors: Kathryn C. Krupinsky, Denise E. Kirschner
Original abstract (English)
Within our synchronous, online global health-focused upper-level microbiology course, we find that students struggle to translate learning to real-world applications. For example, consider the recent measles outbreaks and major events such as the COVID-19 pandemic, which prompt many questions about how basic microbiological information is used by public health professionals. To address these points, we created a simulation-based curriculum that places students in an action role during an infectious disease outbreak. Our stand-alone curriculum walks through a historical measles outbreak that introduces outbreak investigation, community communication, and how these depend on microbiological knowledge. By using breakout groups, students have an opportunity to decide classifications, public messaging, and intervention metrics. We provide students with an outbreak investigation reference worksheet and interweave breakout rooms with didactic vignettes covering background information while revealing actual responses to outbreaks in conjunction with data obtained by responding scientists. Students synthesize material and apply it in real-time, allowing them to exercise critical thinking. Pilot testing in a small, upper-division, virtual classroom suggests that this curriculum has strong potential for increasing mastery of learning objectives and bolstering the relevance of microbiology and public health to popular media.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of students translating microbiological knowledge into real-world applications, particularly in the context of public health emergencies such as the recent measles outbreaks and the COVID-19 pandemic.
Design and population
A simulation-based curriculum was developed for an upper-level microbiology course, focusing on an historical measles outbreak to introduce outbreak investigation, community communication, and the application of microbiological knowledge.
Core methods
The curriculum included a stand-alone simulation that walked through a historical measles outbreak, using breakout groups for decision-making and an outbreak investigation reference worksheet.
Main results
Pilot testing in a small, upper-division, virtual classroom suggested that the curriculum had strong potential for increasing mastery of learning objectives and enhancing the relevance of microbiology and public health to popular media.
Interpretation and novelty
Students were able to synthesize material and apply it in real-time, exercising critical thinking and decision-making skills.
Scientific and public-health significance
The curriculum could potentially improve the application of microbiological knowledge in public health practice, particularly in managing infectious disease outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to a small, upper-division, virtual classroom, and the evidence strength is moderate.

疫情病例线名单离散动态生存分析

Discrete dynamic survival analysis of epidemic case line lists
作者： Sandra Montes-Olivas, Sam Abbott, Sebastian Funk, Mike B. Gravenor, Simon D. W. Frost, Gravenor, M. B., Frost, S. D. W.
摘要中文翻译病例列表记录了发病、康复和住院日期,是传染病 流行病学 中最精细的数据之一,可以提供对 传播 动态的深入了解。动态生存分析 (DSA) 将基于微分方程的确定性模型拟合到行列表中,以估计传染性和传染期等参数。感染期通常不是指数的,将其纳入线表数据的机械模型中通常需要偏微分方程(PDE)方案,该方案可能难以实现并且必须针对每个模型进行定制,这可能会限制它们的使用。在这里,我们提出了离散动态生存分析(DDSA),这是一种基于差分方程的案例行列表的离散时间 DSA。它无需插值即可直接匹配日历日数据,并且其对数似然可通过前向模式自动微分进行微分,从而无需特定于模型的数值基础设施即可实现现成的基于梯度的采样器。非指数感染期通过棚车模型或“阶段装置”的离散模拟来适应。我们将 DDSA 方法应用于甲型流感 (H1N1)、埃博拉和 麻疹 的三个历史 麻疹。传播 参数的 DDSA 估计值接近于已发布的可用分析,这一协议部分反映了共享的建模选择,并且该方法处理缺少发病或恢复日期的不完整行列表。综合实验表明,恢复间隔确定了感染期,而当已知危险人群时,感染时间和最终规模限制了 R0。离散 DSA 为传染病动态的模拟和推断提供了一个简单、可扩展的框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决动态生存分析(DSA)在处理离散时间数据和非指数传染期方面的局限性,这在传染病 流行病学 中很常见。病例列表记录了发病、康复和住院日期,是传染病 流行病学 中最精细的数据之一,可以提供对 传播 动态的深入了解。
设计与对象
该研究将离散动态生存分析(DDSA)应用于甲型H1N1流感、埃博拉病毒和麻疹的三个历史麻疹。DDSA 是一种基于差分方程的案例行列表的离散时间 DSA,它直接匹配日历日数据,无需插值。
核心方法
传播 参数的 DDSA 估计值接近于已发布的可用分析,这一协议部分反映了共享的建模选择,并且该方法处理缺少发病或恢复日期的不完整行列表。综合实验表明,恢复间隔确定了感染期,而当已知危险人群时,感染时间和最终规模限制了 R0。
主要结果
传播 参数的 DDSA 估计值接近已发布的可用分析,并且该方法可以处理缺少发病或恢复日期的不完整行列表。
解释与创新
作者解释说,DDSA 为传染病动态的模拟和推理提供了一个简单、可扩展的框架,它可以通过棚车模型或“阶段设备”的离散模拟来适应非指数传染期。
科研与公卫意义
DDSA可以洞察传播的动态并处理不完整的线表,这对于传染病的监测、诊断和治疗具有重要意义。该方法可以应用于各种历史麻疹,包括麻疹。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括该方法的性能是根据历史 暴发 进行评估的,并且它可能无法很好地推广到其他疾病或环境。由于证据仅具有抽象性质,证据强度中等。

Discrete dynamic survival analysis of epidemic case line lists

Authors: Sandra Montes-Olivas, Sam Abbott, Sebastian Funk, Mike B. Gravenor, Simon D. W. Frost, Gravenor, M. B., Frost, S. D. W.
Original abstract (English)
Case line lists, which record dates of onset, recovery, and hospitalisation, are among the most granular data in infectious disease epidemiology and can provide insights into transmission dynamics. Dynamic survival analysis (DSA) fits a deterministic, differential-equation-based model to line lists to estimate parameters such as infectivity and the infectious period. Infectious periods are usually not exponential, and incorporating this into mechanistic models for line-list data often requires partial differential equation (PDE) schemes that can be difficult to implement and must be customised for each model, which may limit their use. Here, we propose Discrete Dynamic Survival Analysis (DDSA), a discrete-time DSA for case line lists based on difference equations. It matches calendar-day data directly without interpolation, and its log-likelihood is differentiable via forward-mode automatic differentiation, enabling off-the-shelf gradient-based samplers without model-specific numerical infrastructure. Non-exponential infectious periods are accommodated through a boxcar model or a discrete analogue of the "device of stages". We apply the DDSA method to three historical outbreaks of influenza A(H1N1), Ebola, and measles. DDSA estimates of transmission parameters were close to published analyses where these were available, an agreement that partly reflects shared modelling choices, and the method handles incomplete line lists with missing onset or recovery dates. Synthetic experiments show that recovery intervals identify the infectious period, while infection times and final size constrain R0 when the population at risk is known. Discrete DSA provides a simple, extensible framework for simulation and inference of infectious disease dynamics.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limitations of dynamic survival analysis (DSA) in handling discrete-time data and non-exponential infectious periods, which are common in infectious disease epidemiology. Case line lists, which record dates of onset, recovery, and hospitalisation, are among the most granular data in infectious disease epidemiology and can provide insights into transmission dynamics.
Design and population
The study applied Discrete Dynamic Survival Analysis (DDSA) to three historical outbreaks of influenza A(H1N1), Ebola, and measles. DDSA is a discrete-time DSA for case line lists based on difference equations, which matches calendar-day data directly without interpolation.
Core methods
DDSA estimates of transmission parameters were close to published analyses where these were available, an agreement that partly reflects shared modelling choices, and the method handles incomplete line lists with missing onset or recovery dates. Synthetic experiments showed that recovery intervals identify the infectious period, while infection times and final size constrain R0 when the population at risk is known.
Main results
DDSA estimates of transmission parameters were close to published analyses where these were available, and the method handles incomplete line lists with missing onset or recovery dates.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that DDSA provides a simple, extensible framework for simulation and inference of infectious disease dynamics, which can accommodate non-exponential infectious periods through a boxcar model or a discrete analogue of the 'device of stages'.
Scientific and public-health significance
DDSA can provide insights into transmission dynamics and handle incomplete line lists, which is significant for surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment of infectious diseases. The method can be applied to various historical outbreaks, including measles.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the fact that the method's performance was evaluated on historical outbreaks and that it may not generalize well to other diseases or settings. The evidence strength is moderate due to the abstract-only nature of the evidence.

一岁儿童 VAERS 数据库的回顾性分析

Retrospective Analysis of the VAERS Database for Children One Year of Age
作者： Darrell O. Ricke
摘要中文翻译在最近的研究中,针对 1 岁以下婴儿的特定 疫苗 检测到了新的候选安全信号。在此,对 疫苗 不良事件报告系统 (VAERS) 数据库进行了回顾性检查,以寻找针对一岁儿童的可能的新颖候选安全信号。检测到多个 疫苗 的新候选安全信号。确定了两种候选制造违规行为或可能的未知污染物与 1993 年至 1995 年 VAERS 非国产 麻疹、腮腺炎和风疹 (MMR II) 疫苗 批次的不良事件 (AE) 自闭症以及 2019 年至 2024 年非国产 MMRV 批次的 AE 高热相关。候选制造违规行为被命名为 MMR II 的 alpha 和麻疹、腮腺炎、风疹和水痘 (MMRV) ProQuad 疫苗 的测试版。非国内数据中假设的较高水平的 α,MMR II 似乎在 1995 年之后不久的某个时间就被减少或消除了;识别 alpha 将有助于避免未来可能出现的相关 AE。假设非家养 MMRV 疫苗 中 β 的减少可减少高热以及未观察到与家养 MMRV 疫苗 相关的较高发热 AE。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 疫苗 对于一岁儿童的潜在安全信号,之前的研究结果表明特定 疫苗 对于 1 岁以下婴儿有新的候选安全信号。
设计与对象
对一岁儿童的 VAERS 数据库进行了回顾性分析,重点关注与特定 疫苗 相关的可能的新颖候选安全信号。
核心方法
该研究涉及检查 VAERS 数据是否存在与 疫苗 批次相关的不良事件,识别候选生产违规行为或与特定 疫苗 相关的可能未知污染物。
主要结果
在多个 疫苗 中检测到新的候选安全信号,包括与 1993 年至 1995 年 VAERS 非国产 MMR II 疫苗 批次的不良事件自闭症相关的两种候选制造违规行为或可能的未知污染物,以及 2019 年至 2024 年非国产 MMRV 批次的 AE 高热症。
解释与创新
候选制造违规行为被命名为 MMR II 的 alpha 和 MMRV ProQuad 疫苗 的 beta,假设非国内数据中较高水平的 alpha 可能有助于避免未来可能出现的相关 AE。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能会为未来的 监测 和 疫苗 的风险评估策略提供信息,特别是在识别可能与不良事件相关的潜在制造违规行为方面。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于VAERS数据,该数据是回顾性的,可能存在报告偏差,且证据是假设的,需要进一步验证。

Retrospective Analysis of the VAERS Database for Children One Year of Age

Authors: Darrell O. Ricke
Original abstract (English)
In recent studies, novel candidate safety signals were detected for infants younger than 1 year of age for specific vaccines. Herein, the Vaccine Adverse Events Reporting System (VAERS) database was retrospectively examined for possible novel candidate safety signals for children one year of age. Novel candidate safety signals were detected for multiple vaccines. Two candidate manufacturing irregularities or possible unknown contaminants were identified associated with adverse event (AE) autism for VAERS nondomestic Measles, Mumps, and Rubella (MMR II) vaccine lots from 1993 to 1995 and AE hyperpyrexia for nondomestic MMRV lots from 2019 to 2024. The candidate manufacturing irregularities were named alpha for MMR II and beta for Measles, Mumps, Rubella, and Varicella (MMRV) ProQuad vaccines. The hypothesized higher levels of alpha in the nondomestic data, MMR II appear to have been reduced or eliminated sometime soon after 1995; identification of alpha would contribute to future avoidance of possible associated AEs. Reduction of beta in the nondomestic MMRV vaccine is hypothesized to reduce hyperpyrexia and also higher pyrexia AEs not observed associated with domestic MMRV vaccine.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate potential safety signals in vaccines for children one year of age, building on previous findings that indicated novel candidate safety signals for infants younger than 1 year of age for specific vaccines.
Design and population
A retrospective analysis of the VAERS database was conducted for children one year of age, focusing on possible novel candidate safety signals associated with specific vaccines.
Core methods
The study involved examining VAERS data for adverse events linked to vaccine lots, identifying candidate manufacturing irregularities or possible unknown contaminants associated with specific vaccines.
Main results
Novel candidate safety signals were detected for multiple vaccines, including two candidate manufacturing irregularities or possible unknown contaminants associated with adverse event autism for VAERS nondomestic MMR II vaccine lots from 1993 to 1995 and AE hyperpyrexia for nondomestic MMRV lots from 2019 to 2024.
Interpretation and novelty
The candidate manufacturing irregularities were named alpha for MMR II and beta for MMRV ProQuad vaccines, with the hypothesized higher levels of alpha in the nondomestic data potentially contributing to future avoidance of possible associated AEs.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform future surveillance and risk assessment strategies for vaccines, particularly in identifying potential manufacturing irregularities that could be linked to adverse events.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on VAERS data, which is retrospective and may be subject to reporting biases, and the evidence is hypothesized, requiring further verification.

肯尼亚儿童 疫苗接种 覆盖率的常规卫生设施 (KHIS) 与房屋调查 (KDHS) 估计值的比较:一项横断面研究。

Comparison of routine health-facility (KHIS) versus house-survey (KDHS) estimates of childhood vaccination coverage in Kenya: a cross-sectional study.
作者： Cosmas Biwot, Jeanette Dawa, Muema J Mulei, George P Omondi, Damaris Kimonge, Vicky Maiyo, Namu Kimunguyi, Rose Jelang'o, Helen Kiarie, Nyamai Mutono
摘要中文翻译简介:对健康干预措施有效覆盖范围的可靠估计对于跟踪健康目标的进展和为政策决策提供信息至关重要。在常规卫生信息系统有限的环境中,基于人口的家庭调查经常被用作参考标准。目的:本研究比较了肯尼亚基于设施的健康信息系统 (KHIS) 和 2022 年肯尼亚人口健康调查 (KDHS) 的国家和次国家儿童 疫苗接种 覆盖率估计值,以评估两个数据源之间的一致性,并评估常规数据对规划决策的适用性。方法:我们分析了 2020 年 3 月至 2022 年 3 月期间 8178 个卫生机构向 KHIS 报告的 BCG、五价 1、五价 3 和 麻疹-风疹 1 (MR 1) 疫苗接种 数据以及来自 3679 名 12-23 个月儿童的 KDHS 2022 数据。使用二比例 z 检验在国家和县一级比较覆盖率估计值。9-14 个县的覆盖率绝对差异极小(10%)。只有三个县的所有四种抗原的绝对差异<5%;几个县的抗原表现出持续的差异。结论:KHIS 产生了相当一致的国家级儿童 疫苗接种 覆盖率估计值,但县级的巨大且不均匀的差异限制了其在地方决策中的可靠性。需要采取差异化的方法:KHIS 可以支持国家趋势监测,并且可用于具有一致性的县,而持续存在差异的县则需要继续基于调查的验证并有针对性地加强常规数据系统。在县一级,这两个数据源不应被视为可以互换。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在比较肯尼亚基于设施的健康信息系统 (KHIS) 和 2022 年肯尼亚人口健康调查 (KDHS) 的国家和次国家儿童 疫苗接种 覆盖率估计值,以评估两个数据源之间的一致性,并评估常规数据对规划决策的适用性。对健康干预措施有效覆盖范围的可靠估计对于跟踪健康目标的进展情况和为政策决策提供信息至关重要,特别是在常规卫生信息系统有限且经常使用基于人口的家庭调查作为参考标准的环境中。
设计与对象
该研究比较了来自 3679 名 12-23 个月儿童的 KHIS 和 KDHS 2022 数据的国家和次国家儿童 疫苗接种 覆盖率估计值。该分析包括 2020 年 3 月至 2022 年 3 月期间 8178 个卫生机构向 KHIS 报告的 BCG、五价 1、五价 3 和 麻疹-风疹 1 (MR 1) 疫苗接种 数据。
核心方法
使用二比例 z 检验在国家和县一级比较覆盖率估计值。覆盖率的绝对差异分为最小(≤5%)或显着(>5%)。
主要结果
只有三个县的所有四种抗原的绝对差异<5%;几个县的抗原表现出持续的差异。
解释与创新
KHIS 得出了相当一致的国家级儿童 疫苗接种 覆盖率估计值,但县级的巨大且不均匀的差异限制了其在地方决策中的可靠性。需要采取差异化的方法:KHIS 可以支持国家趋势监测,并且可用于具有一致性的县,而持续存在差异的县则需要继续基于调查的验证并有针对性地加强常规数据系统。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,虽然 KHIS 可以提供可靠的国家级估计,但未经进一步验证,它不适合地方决策。这对 疫苗 可预防疾病的 监测、诊断 和 治疗 具有影响,特别是与 ZX​​QTERMEQXZ 密切相关的 麻疹。
局限与证据强度
研究设计的局限性包括潜在的偏差和混杂因素,以及样本量和普遍性。由于研究设计的局限性以及需要在存在持续分歧的县进行进一步验证,证据强度中等。

Comparison of routine health-facility (KHIS) versus house-survey (KDHS) estimates of childhood vaccination coverage in Kenya: a cross-sectional study.

Authors: Cosmas Biwot, Jeanette Dawa, Muema J Mulei, George P Omondi, Damaris Kimonge, Vicky Maiyo, Namu Kimunguyi, Rose Jelang'o, Helen Kiarie, Nyamai Mutono
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Reliable estimates of effective coverage of health interventions are essential for tracking progress towards health goals and informing policy decisions. In settings where routine health information systems are limited, population-based household surveys are frequently used as reference standards. OBJECTIVE: This study compared national and sub-national childhood vaccination coverage estimates from Kenya's facility-based Health Information System (KHIS) and the 2022 Kenya Demographic Health Survey (KDHS) to assess concordance between the two data sources and to evaluate the suitability of routine data for programmatic decision-making. METHODS: We analysed BCG, pentavalent 1, pentavalent 3 and measles-rubella 1 (MR 1) vaccination data reported by 8178 health facilities to KHIS between March 2020 and March 2022 alongside KDHS 2022 data from 3679 children aged 12-23 months. Coverage estimates were compared at national and county levels using a two-proportion z-test. Absolute differences in coverage were categorised as minimal ( 10%) occurred in 9-14 counties. Only three counties showed absolute differences <5% across all four antigens; several counties showed persistent divergence across antigens. CONCLUSION: KHIS yields reasonably concordant national-level estimates of childhood vaccination coverage, but substantial and uneven county-level discrepancies limit its reliability for sub-national decision-making. A differentiated approach is warranted: KHIS can support national trend monitoring and may be usable in counties with demonstrated concordance, whereas counties with persistent divergence require continued survey-based validation and targeted strengthening of routine data systems. The two data sources should not be treated as interchangeable at county level.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to compare national and sub-national childhood vaccination coverage estimates from Kenya's facility-based Health Information System (KHIS) and the 2022 Kenya Demographic Health Survey (KDHS) to assess concordance between the two data sources and to evaluate the suitability of routine data for programmatic decision-making. Reliable estimates of effective coverage of health interventions are essential for tracking progress towards health goals and informing policy decisions, especially in settings where routine health information systems are limited, and population-based household surveys are frequently used as reference standards.
Design and population
The study compared national and sub-national childhood vaccination coverage estimates from KHIS and KDHS 2022 data from 3679 children aged 12-23 months. The analysis included BCG, pentavalent 1, pentavalent 3, and measles-rubella 1 (MR 1) vaccination data reported by 8178 health facilities to KHIS between March 2020 and March 2022.
Core methods
Coverage estimates were compared at national and county levels using a two-proportion z-test. Absolute differences in coverage were categorised as minimal (≤5%) or substantial (>5%).
Main results
Only three counties showed absolute differences <5% across all four antigens; several counties showed persistent divergence across antigens.
Interpretation and novelty
KHIS yields reasonably concordant national-level estimates of childhood vaccination coverage, but substantial and uneven county-level discrepancies limit its reliability for sub-national decision-making. A differentiated approach is warranted: KHIS can support national trend monitoring and may be usable in counties with demonstrated concordance, whereas counties with persistent divergence require continued survey-based validation and targeted strengthening of routine data systems.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that while KHIS can provide reliable national-level estimates, it is not suitable for sub-national decision-making without further validation. This has implications for surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment of vaccine-preventable diseases, particularly measles, which is closely related to the measles virus.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study design limitations include the potential for bias and confounding factors, as well as the sample size and generalisability. The evidence strength is moderate due to the limitations in the study design and the need for further validation in counties with persistent divergence.

第 73 届区域委员会召开后,东地中海区域人民的医疗保健发生了哪些变化?

After Regional Committee 73, what changes in health care for people in the Eastern Mediterranean Region?
作者： Adham Ismail
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了世界卫生组织区域委员会第 73 届会议后东地中海区域 (EMR) 卫生保健系统变革的需求,重点是财务保护和综合疾病消除战略。
设计与对象
该研究是对 EMR 的政策分析,研究了医疗保健融资和疾病消除策略对 17 个国家和地区公共卫生结果的影响。
核心方法
这些方法包括审查来自电子病历的政策决议、卫生支出数据和疾病负担统计数据,包括世卫组织报告和国家卫生规划。
主要结果
结果显示,EMR 中 28% 的 HIV 感染者接受了 治疗,48% 的医疗保健支出由政府资助,在财政保障和疾病消除工作方面存在巨大差距。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解读为强调迫切需要改善财政保护和综合疾病管理战略以应对该地区的健康挑战,并强调政策实施的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,需要协调卫生筹资和疾病消除战略,以改善健康结果并减少电子病历中的财务困难。
局限与证据强度
证据基于政策文件和汇总的健康数据,可能缺乏粒度和直接因果关系,导致信心评估适中。

After Regional Committee 73, what changes in health care for people in the Eastern Mediterranean Region?

Authors: Adham Ismail
Original abstract (English)
There is evidence that this approach delivers results. 659 Editorial EMHJ – Vol. 10 – 2026 After Regional Committee 73, what changes in health care for people in the Eastern Mediterranean Region? Adham Ismail1 1Officer-in-Charge, WHO Regional Office for the Eastern Mediterranean, Cairo, Egypt. Keywords: health financing, financial protection, universal health coverage, multi-communicable disease elimination, health system resilience, health equity, Eastern Mediterranean Citation: Ismail A. After Regional Committee 73, what changes in health care for people in the Eastern Mediterranean Region? East Mediterr Health J. 2026;32(10):659–661. https://doi.org/10.26719/2026.32.10.659. Copyright © Authors 2026. EMHJ is an open-access journal. This paper is available under the Creative Commons Attribution 3.0 IGO licence (CC BY 3.0 IGO; https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/3.0/igo). Meeting in Cairo from 13 to 15 October 2026 for the 73rd Session of the WHO Regional Committee for the Eastern Mediterranean (RC73), health ministers adopted 2 resolutions that could fundamentally change how countries finance health care and tackle communicable diseases across the Eastern Mediterranean Region (EMR). The scale of health needs stands in stark contrast to the resources available to meet them. The EMR is home to just 9% of the world’s population, yet almost half of all people requiring humanitarian assistance live here. The region accounts for less than 2% of the global health spending (1). As of the end of June 2026, 48 disease outbreaks were active across 17 EMR countries and territories, including cholera, measles, dengue, malaria, and vaccine-derived poliovirus (1). Between July 2025 and June 2026, some 632 attacks on health care were recorded, causing 1531 deaths – 86% of all suc...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for changes in health care systems in the Eastern Mediterranean Region (EMR) following the 73rd Session of the WHO Regional Committee, focusing on financial protection and integrated disease elimination strategies.
Design and population
The study is a policy analysis of the EMR, examining the impact of health care financing and disease elimination strategies on public health outcomes across 17 countries and territories.
Core methods
The methods involve reviewing policy resolutions, health expenditure data, and disease burden statistics from the EMR, including WHO reports and national health programs.
Main results
The results show that 28% of people living with HIV in the EMR receive treatment, and 48% of health care expenditure is funded by governments, with significant gaps in financial protection and disease elimination efforts.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as highlighting the urgent need for improved financial protection and integrated disease management strategies to address the region’s health challenges, emphasizing the importance of policy implementation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for coordinated health financing and disease elimination strategies to improve health outcomes and reduce financial hardship in the EMR.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on policy documents and aggregated health data, which may lack granularity and direct causal links, resulting in a moderate confidence assessment.

尼日利亚的人力资本发展和经济增长:自回归分布滞后 (ARDL) 模型方法的应用

Human Capital Development and Economic Growth in Nigeria: An Application of Autoregressive Distributed Lag (ARDL) Model Approach
作者： Chukwuemeka Micheal Ogbonnaya
摘要中文翻译本研究考察了1990年至2020年尼日利亚人力资本发展对经济增长的影响。研究采用事后研究设计,实证考察了以免疫接种为代表的人力资本发展对麻疹、婴儿死亡率率、政府卫生和教育支出以及能源消费总量和以GDP增长率为代表的经济增长的影响。该研究采用了ADF单位根检验;协整的边界测试方法、ARDL 长期和短期估计以及一些后估计测试。因此,单位根检验结果表明,所考虑的变量以不同阶 1 和 0 进行积分。边界检验证实了所讨论的变量之间存在长期关系。长期 ARDL 估计表明,婴儿 死亡率 减少 1% 会导致 GDPG 增加 0.91%。此外,政府教育和卫生支出每增加1%,GDPG分别增加0.23%和0.05%。该研究建议优先投资教育和医疗保健,以降低婴儿 死亡率 率。

问题与背景
本研究考察了 1990 年至 2020 年人力资本发展对尼日利亚经济增长的影响。
设计与对象
本研究采用事后研究设计,实证考察了以免疫麻疹、婴儿死亡率率为代表的人力资本发展、以GDP增速为代表的政府卫生和教育支出以及能源消费总量对经济增长的影响。
核心方法
该研究采用了ADF单位根检验;协整的边界测试方法、ARDL 长期和短期估计以及一些后估计测试。
主要结果
因此,单位根检验结果表明,所考虑的变量以不同阶 1 和 0 进行积分。边界检验证实了所讨论的变量之间存在长期关系。长期 ARDL 估计表明,婴儿 死亡率 减少 1% 会导致 GDPG 增加 0.91%。此外,政府教育和卫生支出每增加1%,GDPG分别增加0.23%和0.05%。
解释与创新
该研究建议优先投资教育和医疗保健,以降低婴儿 死亡率 率。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,通过教育和医疗保健投资改善人力资本可以对尼日利亚的经济增长产生积极影响。
局限与证据强度
这项研究受到人力资本发展和经济增长代理变量使用的限制。证据强度中等。

Human Capital Development and Economic Growth in Nigeria: An Application of Autoregressive Distributed Lag (ARDL) Model Approach

Authors: Chukwuemeka Micheal Ogbonnaya
Original abstract (English)
This study examined the impact of human capital development on economic growth in Nigeria from 1990 to 2020. The study adopted an ex post facto research design, to make an empirical investigation on the impact of human capital development proxy by Immunization against measles, infant mortality rate, and government expenditure on health and education as well as total energy consumption and on economic growth proxy by the growth rate of GDP. The study employed the ADF unit root test; the bounds test approach to cointegration, ARDL long and Short-run estimations as well as some post-estimation tests. Accordingly, the unit root test result showed that the variables under consideration are integrated at different orders 1 and 0. The bound test confirmed the presence of a long-run relationship among the variables under discourse. The ARDL estimation in the long run showed that a 1% decrease in infant mortality resulted in a 0.91% increase in GDPG. Also, a 1% increase in government expenditure on education and health increased GDPG by 0.23% and 0.05% respectively. The study recommended the prioritization of investment in education and healthcare to reduce infant mortality rates.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study examined the impact of human capital development on economic growth in Nigeria from 1990 to 2020.
Design and population
The study adopted an ex post facto research design, to make an empirical investigation on the impact of human capital development proxy by Immunization against measles, infant mortality rate, and government expenditure on health and education as well as total energy consumption and on economic growth proxy by the growth rate of GDP.
Core methods
The study employed the ADF unit root test; the bounds test approach to cointegration, ARDL long and Short-run estimations as well as some post-estimation tests.
Main results
Accordingly, the unit root test result showed that the variables under consideration are integrated at different orders 1 and 0. The bound test confirmed the presence of a long-run relationship among the variables under discourse. The ARDL estimation in the long run showed that a 1% decrease in infant mortality resulted in a 0.91% increase in GDPG. Also, a 1% increase in government expenditure on education and health increased GDPG by 0.23% and 0.05% respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The study recommended the prioritization of investment in education and healthcare to reduce infant mortality rates.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that improving human capital through investments in education and healthcare can positively impact economic growth in Nigeria.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of proxy variables for human capital development and economic growth. The evidence strength is moderate.

传染病多区域离散时间链二项式模型传播

A multi-region discrete time chain binomial model for infectious disease transmission
作者： Sinha, P. K., Mukhopadhyay, S.
摘要中文翻译传统的传染病数学模型常常忽视疾病的空间传播,只关注局部的传播。然而,已观察到各种疾病的空间传播主要是由于感染者从一个地区移动到另一地区。在这项工作中,我们提出了一个多区域离散时间链二项式框架来模拟相邻区域感染时间序列之间的依赖关系。所提出的模型基于流行病学原理,即新的感染是由易感者和先前感染者之间的相互作用引起的。为了加强统计公式,每个空间单元内新感染的概率通过链接函数进行建模,允许其依赖于滞后的局部 发病率、区域间 传播 效应和区域特定的协变量。该框架还允许通过 AR(p) 规范实现高阶时间依赖性。为了随着区域数量的增加而降低模型的复杂性,通过使用空间邻接矩阵和 k 最近邻结构来施加稀疏性。为了估计这些多区域模型,提出了一种适当的似然函数最大化方法。基于预测可能性的方法用于生成连接空间单元中疾病计数的预测。对两个真实 麻疹 数据集的深入分析和结合季节性模式、连接区域的不同权重矩阵以及不同干预策略的模拟研究用于激发所提出的建模框架,并证明其再现真实 麻疹 动态的能力。虽然模拟研究证明了一般 AR(p) 方法的适用性,但 麻疹 的实际数据分析表明 AR(1) 模型足以捕获时间依赖性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
传统的传染病数学模型常常忽视疾病的空间传播,只关注局部的传播。然而,已观察到各种疾病的空间传播主要是由于感染者从一个地区移动到另一地区。
设计与对象
在这项工作中,我们提出了一个多区域离散时间链二项式框架来模拟相邻区域感染时间序列之间的依赖关系。
核心方法
所提出的模型基于流行病学原理,即新的感染是由易感者和先前感染者之间的相互作用引起的。为了加强统计公式,每个空间单元内新感染的概率通过链接函数进行建模,允许其依赖于滞后的局部 发病率、区域间 传播 效应和区域特定的协变量。为了随着区域数量的增加而降低模型的复杂性,通过使用空间邻接矩阵和 k 最近邻结构来施加稀疏性。为了估计这些多区域模型,提出了一种适当的似然函数最大化方法。
主要结果
对两个真实 麻疹 数据集的深入分析和结合季节性模式、连接区域的不同权重矩阵以及不同干预策略的模拟研究用于激发所提出的建模框架,并证明其再现真实 麻疹 动态的能力。虽然模拟研究证明了一般 AR(p) 方法的适用性,但 麻疹 的实际数据分析表明 AR(1) 模型足以捕获时间依赖性。
解释与创新
该框架允许通过 AR(p) 规范实现高阶时间依赖性,并通过使用稀疏性随着区域数量的增加而降低模型的复杂性。
科研与公卫意义
该框架可以重现真实的 暴发 动态,可用于预测连接空间单元中的疾病计数。这对于理解和预测 麻疹 在不同地区的传播尤其重要。
局限与证据强度
所提出的模型依赖于这样的假设:新的感染是由易感者和先前感染者之间的相互作用引起的。麻疹 的真实数据分析表明,AR(1) 模型足以捕获时间依赖性,但该模型的性能可能会受到现实世界 传播 动力学的复杂性的限制。

A multi-region discrete time chain binomial model for infectious disease transmission

Authors: Sinha, P. K., Mukhopadhyay, S.
Original abstract (English)
Conventional mathematical models of infectious diseases often overlook the spatial spread of the disease, focusing only on local transmission. However, spatial propagation of various diseases has been observed mainly due to the movement of infectious individuals from one region to another. In this work, we propose a multiregion discrete-time chain binomial framework to model dependencies between infection time series from neighboring regions. The proposed model is based on the epidemiological principle that new infections arise from interactions between susceptible and previously infected individuals. To strengthen the statistical formulation, the probability of new infections within each spatial unit is modeled through a link function, by allowing it to depend on lagged local incidence, inter-regional transmission effects, and region-specific covariates. The framework also allows for higher-order temporal dependence through an AR(p) specification. To reduce complexity of the model as the number of regions increases, sparsity is imposed by using spatial contiguity matrices and k-nearest-neighbor structures. For estimating these multiregion models, an appropriate likelihood function maximization approach is proposed. A predictive likelihood based method is used to generate forecasts of disease counts in connected spatial units. In-depth analyses of two real measles datasets and simulation studies incorporating seasonal patterns, different weight matrices across connected regions, and varying intervention strategies are used to motivate the proposed modeling framework and demonstrate its ability to reproduce realistic outbreak dynamics. While the simulation studies demonstrate applicability of the general AR(p) approach, the real data analysis of measles shows that an AR(1) model is adequate to capture the temporal dependence.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Conventional mathematical models of infectious diseases often overlook the spatial spread of the disease, focusing only on local transmission. However, spatial propagation of various diseases has been observed mainly due to the movement of infectious individuals from one region to another.
Design and population
In this work, we propose a multiregion discrete-time chain binomial framework to model dependencies between infection time series from neighboring regions.
Core methods
The proposed model is based on the epidemiological principle that new infections arise from interactions between susceptible and previously infected individuals. To strengthen the statistical formulation, the probability of new infections within each spatial unit is modeled through a link function, by allowing it to depend on lagged local incidence, inter-regional transmission effects, and region-specific covariates. To reduce complexity of the model as the number of regions increases, sparsity is imposed by using spatial contiguity matrices and k-nearest-neighbor structures. For estimating these multiregion models, an appropriate likelihood function maximization approach is proposed.
Main results
In-depth analyses of two real measles datasets and simulation studies incorporating seasonal patterns, different weight matrices across connected regions, and varying intervention strategies are used to motivate the proposed modeling framework and demonstrate its ability to reproduce realistic outbreak dynamics. While the simulation studies demonstrate applicability of the general AR(p) approach, the real data analysis of measles shows that an AR(1) model is adequate to capture the temporal dependence.
Interpretation and novelty
The framework allows for higher-order temporal dependence through an AR(p) specification and reduces complexity of the model as the number of regions increases by using sparsity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The framework can reproduce realistic outbreak dynamics and is useful for forecasting disease counts in connected spatial units. It is particularly relevant for understanding and predicting the spread of measles in different regions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The proposed model relies on the assumption that new infections arise from interactions between susceptible and previously infected individuals. The real data analysis of measles shows that an AR(1) model is adequate to capture the temporal dependence, but the model's performance may be limited by the complexity of real-world transmission dynamics.

对尼日利亚受冲突影响的博尔诺州双强度 麻疹 监测 系统进行评估。

Evaluation of a dual-intensity measles surveillance system in conflict-affected Borno State, Nigeria.
作者： Taofik Ademola Oloyede, Bello Umar, Mohammed Abba Mustapha, Fatimah Lawan Bukar, Usman Mukhtar Gajiram, Ahmad Aliyu Mohammad, Aisha Aliyu Abulfathi
摘要中文翻译简介:由于不安全、实验室准入有限和流行病激增,在脆弱、受冲突影响的环境中维持 麻疹 监测 仍然具有挑战性。尼日利亚博尔诺州运行双强度 监测 框架,将基于实验室确诊病例的 监测 (CBS-Lab) 与流行病学相关的 暴发 报告 (CBS-Epi) 相结合。我们评估了 2020 年至 2024 年的系统性能、时空 监测 模式和操作限制。方法:我们使用美国疾病控制和预防中心更新的指南进行了程序化 监测 系统评估,根据 WHO AFRO 基准评估系统属性。该评估使用了回顾性 麻疹 监测 数据(28 370 例疑似病例)和对 麻疹 官员评估感知和操作领域的横断面调查(31/58;53% 的回应),并辅以背景政策文件审查。使用 Spearman 等级相关性分析 监测 流之间的时空对齐。结果:CBS-Lab 占通知的 6.1% (n=1725),作为哨兵子集,完整性≥98%,阳性预测值 47.8%,48 小时内收集到 88.4% 的样本,但只有 5% 的病例达到 <10 天的周转标准。CBS-Epi (93.9%;n=26 645) 保持 ≥99.8% 的完整性,尽管代表性受到受冲突影响地方政府地区交通不便的限制,但在 2021 年和 2023 年达到了流行病高峰。由于协议设计,流之间没有发生病例记录重叠。总体时间 (ρ=0.69) 和地理 (ρ=0.65) 协变很强 (p<0.001)。实验室采样强度与地区病例负担呈负相关(ρ=-0.64;p<0.001),表明基于风险的优先顺序。尽管不安全、交通和资金有限,但可接受性仍然很高。结论:在博尔诺州的具体运作背景下,双强度 死亡率 实现了可操作的有用的流行病情报,并在长期冲突的情况下保持了 死亡率 减少阶段的表现。这些发现表明,简化的 暴发 报告和有针对性的实验室确认的务实结合可能代表了在相当脆弱的环境中可行的 监测 方法,尽管需要根据具体情况进行调整。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
我们评估了 2020 年至 2024 年的系统性能、时空 监测 模式和操作约束。
证据基础与方法
我们使用美国疾病控制和预防中心更新的指南进行了程序化的 监测 系统评估,根据 WHO AFRO 基准评估系统属性。
共识与结论
CBS-Lab 代表了 6.1% (n=1725) 的通知,作为哨兵子集,完整性≥98%,阳性预测值 47.8%,48 小时内收集了 88.4% 的样本,但只有 5% 的病例达到 <10 天的周转标准。CBS-Epi (93.9%;n=26 645) 保持了 ≥99.8% 的完整性,并在 2019 年捕获了流行病高峰。2021 年和 2023 年,尽管代表性受到受冲突影响地方政府地区地理交通不便的限制。
争议与缺口
这些发现表明,简化的 暴发 报告和有针对性的实验室确认的务实结合可能代表了在相当脆弱的环境中可行的 监测 方法,尽管需要根据具体情况进行调整。
科研与实践启示
尼日利亚博尔诺州运行双强度 监测 框架,将基于实验室确诊病例的 监测 (CBS-Lab) 与流行病学相关的 暴发 报告 (CBS-Epi) 相结合。

Evaluation of a dual-intensity measles surveillance system in conflict-affected Borno State, Nigeria.

Authors: Taofik Ademola Oloyede, Bello Umar, Mohammed Abba Mustapha, Fatimah Lawan Bukar, Usman Mukhtar Gajiram, Ahmad Aliyu Mohammad, Aisha Aliyu Abulfathi
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Maintaining measles surveillance in fragile, conflict-affected settings remains challenging due to insecurity, limited laboratory access and epidemic surges. Borno State, Nigeria, operates a dual-intensity surveillance framework combining laboratory-confirmed case-based surveillance (CBS-Lab) with epidemiologically linked outbreak reporting (CBS-Epi). We evaluated system performance, spatiotemporal surveillance patterns and operational constraints from 2020 to 2024. METHODS: We conducted a programmatic surveillance system evaluation using the US Centers for Disease Control and Prevention updated guidelines, assessing system attributes against WHO AFRO benchmarks. The evaluation used retrospective measles surveillance data (28 370 suspected cases) and a cross-sectional survey of surveillance officers assessing perceptual and operational domains (31/58; 53% response), supplemented by a contextual policy document review. Spatiotemporal alignment between surveillance streams was analysed using Spearman's rank correlation. RESULTS: CBS-Lab represented 6.1% (n=1725) of notifications, functioning as a sentinel subset with ≥98% completeness, a 47.8% positive predictive value and 88.4% specimen collection within 48 hours, though only 5% of cases met the <10-day turnaround standard.CBS-Epi (93.9%; n=26 645) maintained ≥99.8% completeness and captured epidemic peaks in 2021 and 2023, though representativeness was constrained by geographic inaccessibility in conflict-affected local government areas. No case-record overlap occurred between streams due to protocol design. Aggregate temporal (ρ=0.69) and geographic (ρ=0.65) co-variations were strong (p<0.001). Laboratory sampling intensity was inversely correlated with district case burden (ρ=-0.64; p<0.001), indicating risk-based prioritisation. Acceptability remained high despite insecurity, transport and funding constraints. CONCLUSION: Within the specific operational context of Borno State, dual-intensity surveillance enabled operationally useful epidemic intelligence and maintained mortality-reduction phase performance despite protracted conflict. These findings suggest that the pragmatic combination of simplified outbreak reporting and targeted laboratory confirmation may represent a feasible surveillance approach in comparable fragile settings, though contextual adaptation would be required.

We evaluated system performance, spatiotemporal surveillance patterns and operational constraints from 2020 to 2024.

Consensus and conclusions
CBS-Lab represented 6.1% (n=1725) of notifications, functioning as a sentinel subset with ≥98% completeness, a 47.8% positive predictive value and 88.4% specimen collection within 48 hours, though only 5% of cases met the <10-day turnaround standard.CBS-Epi (93.9%; n=26 645) maintained ≥99.8% completeness and captured epidemic peaks in 2021 and 2023, though representativeness was constrained by geographic inaccessibility in conflict-affected local government areas.
Controversies and gaps
These findings suggest that the pragmatic combination of simplified outbreak reporting and targeted laboratory confirmation may represent a feasible surveillance approach in comparable fragile settings, though contextual adaptation would be required.
Research and practice implications
Borno State, Nigeria, operates a dual-intensity surveillance framework combining laboratory-confirmed case-based surveillance (CBS-Lab) with epidemiologically linked outbreak reporting (CBS-Epi).

麻疹 在意大利卷土重来:2023 年 10 月至 2025 年 3 月期间病毒 传播、监测 表现以及 疫苗接种 在国家和地区层面覆盖的时间模式分析

Measles resurgence in Italy: analysis of temporal patterns of viral transmission, surveillance performance, and vaccination coverage at national and regional levels, October 2023 to March 2025
作者： Valentina Baccolini, Antonio Sciurti, Erika Renzi, Azzurra Massimi, Giulia Bonanno, Serena Brunone, Benedetta Dellisanti, Marianna Riccio, Romina Pausilli, Chiara Tommasino
摘要中文翻译背景 随着 COVID-19 大流行后在消除 麻疹 方面取得重大进展,意大利的 麻疹 病例在 2024 年显着增加。目的我们的目标是描述 2023 年 10 月至 2025 年 3 月意大利的 麻疹 流行病学,并评估 疫苗接种 的覆盖范围、麻疹 在国家和地区层面的表现以及时间传播 的模式。方法对麻疹和暴发病例数进行回顾。在国家和地区层面描述了 疫苗接种 覆盖范围和 监测 指标。麻疹 变体按基因型和独特序列标识符 (DSId) 进行分类。在国家和地区范围内评估了 麻疹 变体的时间 传播 模式,以确定持续的 传播 链(≥连续 12 个月)。结果 共报告 麻疹 病例 1,311 例,其中 麻疹 病例 178 例。病例中位年龄为30.9岁(IQR:15.6-39.8);女性 601 人(45.8%),男性 710 人(54.2%)。只有 3.1% (38/1,230) 完全接种了疫苗。2024年,全国第一剂疫苗接种覆盖率增至94.77%(95% CI:94.70-94.84%),而第二剂覆盖率下降至84.45%(95% CI:84.35-84.55%)。在所有病例中,12.1% (n = 158) 为输入/输入相关病例,64.1% (n = 841) 来源不明,52.8% 有可用的 DSId 数据 (n = 692),区域异质性较高。已识别出 25 个 麻疹 变体。一种变体(D8-5963)显示全国 传播 持续超过 12 个月,符合重新建立地方性 传播 的标准,但没有任何一个地区显示不间断 ≥ 12 个月的循环。结论 麻疹 在意大利的复苏以持续的免疫差距、异质性的 麻疹 表现以及 D8 变种在全国范围内的持续流通为标志,表明其接近消除状态的丧失。迫切需要加强 疫苗接种 策略并增强 监测 分子,以恢复 麻疹 消除的进展。

范围与问题
背景 随着 COVID-19 大流行后在消除 麻疹 方面取得重大进展,意大利的 麻疹 病例在 2024 年显着增加。目的我们的目标是描述 2023 年 10 月至 2025 年 3 月意大利的 麻疹 流行病学,并评估 疫苗接种 的覆盖范围、麻疹 在国家和地区层面的表现以及时间传播 的模式。
证据基础与方法
方法对麻疹和暴发病例数进行回顾。在国家和地区范围内评估了 麻疹 变体的时间 传播 模式,以确定持续的 传播 链(≥连续 12 个月)。
共识与结论
结果 共报告 麻疹 病例 1,311 例,其中 麻疹 病例 178 例。一种变体(D8-5963)显示全国 传播 持续超过 12 个月,符合重新建立地方性 传播 的标准,但没有任何一个地区显示不间断 ≥ 12 个月的循环。结论 麻疹 在意大利的复苏以持续的免疫差距、异质性的 麻疹 表现以及 D8 变种在全国范围内的持续流通为标志,表明其接近消除状态的丧失。

在所有病例中,12.1% (n = 158) 为输入/输入相关病例,64.1% (n = 841) 来源不明,52.8% 有可用的 DSId 数据 (n = 692),区域异质性较高。提供的证据未报告与 麻疹 复苏分析相关的具体限制或证据强度。
科研与实践启示
在国家和地区层面描述了 疫苗接种 覆盖范围和 监测 指标。迫切需要加强 疫苗接种 策略并增强 监测 分子,以恢复 麻疹 消除的进展。

Measles resurgence in Italy: analysis of temporal patterns of viral transmission, surveillance performance, and vaccination coverage at national and regional levels, October 2023 to March 2025

Authors: Valentina Baccolini, Antonio Sciurti, Erika Renzi, Azzurra Massimi, Giulia Bonanno, Serena Brunone, Benedetta Dellisanti, Marianna Riccio, Romina Pausilli, Chiara Tommasino
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Following substantial progress towards measles elimination after the COVID-19 pandemic, Italy experienced a marked increase in measles cases in 2024. AIM Our aim was to describe measles epidemiology in Italy, October 2023–March 2025, and assess vaccination coverage, surveillance performance at national and regional levels, and temporal patterns of transmission. METHODS Numbers of measles cases and outbreaks were reviewed. Vaccination coverage and surveillance indicators were described at national and regional levels. Measles variants were classified by genotype and Distinct Sequence Identifier (DSId). Temporal transmission patterns of measles variants were assessed nationally and regionally to identify sustained transmission chains (≥ 12 consecutive months). RESULTS In total, 1,311 measles cases were reported, with 178 outbreaks. Cases’ median age was 30.9 years (IQR: 15.6–39.8); 601 were female (45.8%) and 710 male (54.2%). Only 3.1% (38/1,230) were fully vaccinated. In 2024, the first-dose vaccination coverage increased to 94.77% (95% CI: 94.70–94.84%) nationally, while second-dose coverage declined to 84.45% (95% CI: 84.35–84.55%). Among all cases, 12.1% (n = 158) were imported/importation-related, 64.1% (n = 841) were of unknown origin and 52.8% had DSId data available (n = 692), with high regional heterogeneity. Twenty-five measles variants were identified. One variant (D8–5963) showed sustained national transmission for over 12 months, meeting criteria for re-established endemic transmission, although no single region showed uninterrupted ≥ 12-month circulation. CONCLUSION Measles resurgence in Italy was marked by persistent immunity gaps, heterogeneous surveillance performance, and sustained national circulation of a D8 variant, indicating loss of near-elimination status. Strengthening vaccination strategies and enhancing molecular surveillance is urgently needed to restore progress towards measles elimination.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
BACKGROUND Following substantial progress towards measles elimination after the COVID-19 pandemic, Italy experienced a marked increase in measles cases in 2024. AIM Our aim was to describe measles epidemiology in Italy, October 2023–March 2025, and assess vaccination coverage, surveillance performance at national and regional levels, and temporal patterns of transmission.
Evidence base and method
METHODS Numbers of measles cases and outbreaks were reviewed. Temporal transmission patterns of measles variants were assessed nationally and regionally to identify sustained transmission chains (≥ 12 consecutive months).
Consensus and conclusions
RESULTS In total, 1,311 measles cases were reported, with 178 outbreaks. One variant (D8–5963) showed sustained national transmission for over 12 months, meeting criteria for re-established endemic transmission, although no single region showed uninterrupted ≥ 12-month circulation. CONCLUSION Measles resurgence in Italy was marked by persistent immunity gaps, heterogeneous surveillance performance, and sustained national circulation of a D8 variant, indicating loss of near-elimination status.
Controversies and gaps
Among all cases, 12.1% (n = 158) were imported/importation-related, 64.1% (n = 841) were of unknown origin and 52.8% had DSId data available (n = 692), with high regional heterogeneity. The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations or evidence strength related to the measles resurgence analysis.
Research and practice implications
Vaccination coverage and surveillance indicators were described at national and regional levels. Strengthening vaccination strategies and enhancing molecular surveillance is urgently needed to restore progress towards measles elimination.

孟加拉国,居住在洪水易发地区与不完整童年 疫苗接种 之间的关联。

Association between residence in flood-prone areas and incomplete childhood vaccination in Bangladesh.
作者： Emilie Schwarz, Anna Dimitrova, Francois Rerolle, Tarik Benmarhnia, Kévin Jean
摘要中文翻译与气候变化和变异密切相关的洪水事件可能会扰乱社区和卫生系统。疫苗接种 是一种高效的公共卫生干预措施,在全球预防儿童 死亡率 方面发挥着至关重要的作用。然而,洪水多发的居住地与幼儿 疫苗接种 之间的关系仍未得到充分探索。本研究将全球洪水数据库的洪水暴露数据与孟加拉国五波人口和健康调查(2004-2018 年)结合起来,其中包括三岁以下儿童的 疫苗接种 历史记录。使用地理坐标,将家庭与空间洪水数据进行匹配,以确定儿童是否生活在洪水易发地区。应用广义估计方程,考虑地理聚类,评估洪水暴露与不完整 疫苗接种 整体以及 疫苗 类型和剂量之间的关联。样本总数为19,021名儿童,其中1岁以上儿童12,930名,纳入主要分析。在一年以上的人中,16% 的人生活在洪水易发地区,84% 的人已完全接种疫苗。洪水易发地区的儿童错过白喉-破伤风-百日咳 (DTP;PR = 1.34,CI: 1.11-1.62) 疫苗、卡介苗 (BCG;PR = 1.24,CI: 1.02-1.50) 疫苗 和口服脊髓灰质炎的风险较高疫苗(OPV;PR = 1.32,CI:1.10-1.59)疫苗。对于单独包含 麻疹 的 疫苗(MCV;PR = 1.11,CI:0.92-1.35)或所有四个 疫苗 组合(PR = 1.09,CI:0.91-1.30),关联并不精确。这些发现强调,洪水易发地区的居住与某些 疫苗 之间存在关联,强调了在洪水期间保持获得常规免疫服务的重要性,预计洪水将随着气候变化而增加。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究调查了孟加拉国洪水易发地区的居住与不完整童年 麻疹病毒 之间的关联,重点关注 麻疹病毒 和相关实体。核心问题是洪水暴露是否会影响 麻疹 和其他 疫苗 的 疫苗接种 完成率。
证据基础与方法
该审查将全球洪水数据库的洪水暴露数据与孟加拉国五波人口和健康调查(2004-2018)结合起来,包括三岁以下儿童的 疫苗接种 历史。地理坐标用于将家庭与空间洪水数据相匹配,并应用广义估计方程来解释地理聚类。
共识与结论
生活在洪水易发地区的儿童漏检白喉-破伤风-百日咳(DTP;PR = 1.34,CI:1.11-1.62)、卡介苗(BCG;PR = 1.24,CI:1.02-1.50)和口服脊髓灰质炎 疫苗(OPV;PR = 1.32,CI:CI: 1.10-1.59) 疫苗。然而,对于单独包含 麻疹 的 疫苗(MCV;PR = 1.11,CI:0.92-1.35)或所有四个 疫苗 组合(PR = 1.09,CI:0.91-1.30),这种关联并不精确。
争议与缺口
该研究没有单独提供包含 麻疹 的 疫苗 或所有四个 疫苗 组合的精确关联,这表明存在潜在的证据差距。研究结果表明,洪水易发地区可能会影响某些 疫苗 的 疫苗接种 费率,但其机制仍不清楚。
科研与实践启示
在洪水期间维持常规免疫服务至关重要,因为预计这些事件会随着气候变化而增加。需要进一步研究来了解洪水暴露对 麻疹 疫苗接种 率的具体影响,并制定有针对性的干预措施。

Association between residence in flood-prone areas and incomplete childhood vaccination in Bangladesh.

Authors: Emilie Schwarz, Anna Dimitrova, Francois Rerolle, Tarik Benmarhnia, Kévin Jean
Original abstract (English)
Flooding events, closely linked to climate change and variability, can disrupt communities and health systems. Vaccination, a highly effective public health intervention, plays a crucial role in preventing child mortality worldwide. However, the relationship between flood-prone residence and early childhood vaccination remains underexplored. This study combines flood exposure data from the Global Flood Database with five waves of Demographic and Health Surveys (2004-2018) in Bangladesh, including vaccination histories for children under three years. Using geographic coordinates, households were matched to spatial flood data to identify whether children lived in flood-prone areas. Generalized Estimating Equations were applied, accounting for geographic clustering, to assess the association between flood exposure and incomplete vaccination overall, as well as by vaccine type and dose. The total sample included 19,021 children, with 12,930 of them being over 1 years old and included in the main analysis. Of those over one year, 16% were defined as living in flood-prone areas, and 84% were fully vaccinated. Children in flood-prone areas had a higher risk of missing the diphtheria-tetanus-pertussis (DTP; PR = 1.34, CI: 1.11-1.62) vaccine, the bacille Calmette-Guerin (BCG; PR = 1.24, CI: 1.02-1.50) vaccine, and the oral polio vaccine (OPV; PR = 1.32, CI: 1.10-1.59) vaccines. Associations were not precise for the measles-containing vaccine (MCV; PR = 1.11, CI: 0.92-1.35) alone, nor for all four vaccines combined (PR = 1.09, CI: 0.91-1.30). These findings highlight that there is an association between residence in flood prone areas and certain vaccines, highlighting the importance of maintaining access to routine immunization services during floods, which are expected to increase with climate change.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study investigates the association between residence in flood-prone areas and incomplete childhood vaccination in Bangladesh, focusing on the measles virus and related entities. The central question is whether flood exposure affects vaccination completion rates for measles and other vaccines.
Evidence base and method
The review combines flood exposure data from the Global Flood Database with five waves of Demographic and Health Surveys (2004-2018) in Bangladesh, including vaccination histories for children under three years. Geographic coordinates were used to match households to spatial flood data, and Generalized Estimating Equations were applied to account for geographic clustering.
Consensus and conclusions
Children living in flood-prone areas had a higher risk of missing the diphtheria-tetanus-pertussis (DTP; PR = 1.34, CI: 1.11-1.62), bacille Calmette-Guerin (BCG; PR = 1.24, CI: 1.02-1.50), and oral polio vaccine (OPV; PR = 1.32, CI: 1.10-1.59) vaccines. However, the association was not precise for the measles-containing vaccine (MCV; PR = 1.11, CI: 0.92-1.35) alone, nor for all four vaccines combined (PR = 1.09, CI: 0.91-1.30).
Controversies and gaps
The study does not provide precise associations for the measles-containing vaccine alone or for all four vaccines combined, indicating a potential evidence gap. The findings suggest that flood-prone areas may impact vaccination rates for certain vaccines, but the mechanisms remain unclear.
Research and practice implications
Maintaining access to routine immunization services during floods is crucial, as these events are expected to increase with climate change. Further research is needed to understand the specific impacts of flood exposure on measles vaccination rates and to develop targeted interventions.
